However, Muon's optimizer state is more sensitive to quantization errors: because the orthogonalization discards the magnitudes of singular values and retains only directional information, even small quantization errors in singular vector directions are amplified in the update. In this work, we propose MuonQ, a low-b","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T01:31:32+00:00","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2605_11396.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是 Muon 优化器在低比特状态量化下为什么比 Adam 更难做，以及怎样把它真正压到纯 4-bit 还能维持训练质量。关键矛盾不在数值重构误差本身，而在 Muon 的更新只保留动量矩阵的方向信息：极分解会把奇异值幅度抹平，只留下奇异向量张成的方向。因此，哪怕量化误差在元素级看起来很小，只要它扰动了奇异向量方向，进入 polar 后就会被完整保留下来，并直接影响参数更新。论文围绕这个机制提出“directional fidelity optimization”，把优化目标从传统的数值保真改成方向保真。","importance":"对预训练研究，这篇的分量是比较实的。原因有三点。第一，它触及训练内存这个硬约束，而且不是泛泛讨论 8-bit optimizer state，而是针对 Muon 这种新优化器给出纯 4-bit 方案。Muon 的吸引力在于收敛快、算子结构适合大矩阵更新，但如果状态内存压不下去，训练侧收益会被削弱。第二，它给出一个很有代表性的结论：优化器状态量化不能只看 element-wise reconstruction，必须看优化器真正使用了状态的哪一部分信息。对 Adam，幅度统计很重要；对 Muon，方向结构更重要。这个判断对后续设计其他低精度训练算法也有启发。第三，论文把“优化器机制—量化误差形态—训练稳定性”这条链路讲得比较完整，不只是工程 patch。对做大规模 pretrain 的人，这类工作比单纯的压缩技巧更有参考价值，因为它直接关系到训练配方能否在固定显存预算下扩展模型或 batch size。","technical_approach":"方法由三部分组成，都是围绕“不要让量化误差形成系统性的方向偏移”。\n\n第一部分是 pre-quantization normalization。作者观察到，如果每一步都直接量化动量或梯度，量化误差的绝对尺度会随着状态幅值变化而变化，长期累积后会在某些主方向上形成偏置。Muon 又恰好只保留方向，这种偏置会被放大。为此，MuonQ 在量化前先做归一化，让每一步引入的误差幅度更均匀。这样累积误差更接近各向同性噪声，而不是沿某个奇异方向持续漂移。这个设计的重点不是减小单步 MSE，而是控制多步误差的几何形态。\n\n第二部分是 structural decomposition。论文指出，直接对完整动量矩阵做低比特量化，经过 orthogonalization 后，量化噪声会转化为奇异向量旋转，进而破坏 polar(M) 的方向。MuonQ 的做法是先把动量做结构分解，再分别量化奇异因子，使量化误差更多落在奇异值幅度上，而不是奇异向量方向上。因为 Muon 最终会把奇异值全部归一化掉，幅度误差相对不敏感，方向误差才是致命的。论文还给了 truncated top-k 变体，在保留主要结构的同时进一步压缩内存和计算。这里的关键不是“做了 SVD”这么简单，而是利用 Muon 更新对谱幅度不敏感这一特性，主动把误差重分配到不重要的子空间。\n\n第三部分是 μ-law companding quantization。作者认为 Muon 对零附近分辨率要求更高，因为极分解后各奇异方向被等权对待，很多小幅值成分并不会像 Adam 那样自然被二阶统计压低权重。均匀量化在零附近的 bin 不够密，会损伤这些细粒度方向信息。于是先做 companding，把动态范围压缩后再量化，把更多码点分配给稠密区域，尤其是接近零的部分。这和音频压缩里的 μ-law 思路一致，但这里服务的是优化器状态的方向保真。\n\n三部分合起来，形成一个比较完整的设计：归一化控制误差累积形态，结构分解控制误差落点，companding 控制低幅值区域分辨率。与已有 Muon 量化工作相比，区别在于它不是混合精度保留主奇异分量，也不是单纯换更细的 blockwise quantizer，而是从 Muon 的 polar 更新机制反推量化目标函数。","experiments":"实验结论很集中：在 GPT-style 和 LLaMA-style 预训练设置上，MuonQ 的纯 4-bit optimizer state 能把训练 loss 和下游精度做到接近全精度 Muon，同时把 optimizer state memory 压到最高 7.3× 的节省。这个结果重要，因为此前文中给出的背景是：8-bit Muon 基本可行，但 4-bit 纯量化会明显退化，已有方法往往需要把主奇异分量保留在更高精度。MuonQ 试图把这个缺口补上。\n\n从全文结构推断，关键实验应包括以下几类。第一类是训练曲线对比：full-precision Muon、naive 4-bit、已有 8-bit 或 mixed-precision 基线、以及 MuonQ。这里要看 loss 是否全程贴近，而不是只看末点。第二类是下游评测：预训练后在常见语言任务上的 zero-shot 或 few-shot 表现，验证低比特状态没有把泛化能力换掉。第三类是组件消融：只做 normalization、只做 decomposition、只做 companding，以及 top-k 截断比例变化。若论文做得扎实，这些消融会显示三部分是互补关系，而不是某一个技巧单独贡献全部收益。第四类是误差分析：例如量化后奇异向量偏转、polar update 偏差、不同 bit-width 下方向相似度等，这类图最能支撑“directional fidelity”这个中心论点。\n\n当前可确认的硬信息包括：实验覆盖 GPT-style 与 LLaMA-style 模型；目标是 pure 4-bit Muon state quantization；结果是训练损失和下游任务精度接近 full-precision Muon；optimizer state memory 最多减少 7.3×。如果阅读全文，建议把模型规模、训练 token 数、量化粒度、top-k 设置和与 Adam/Muon 基线的具体差值逐项记下来，因为这些细节决定方法是否真能迁移到大规模训练。","reading_guide":"这篇值得精读，顺序很重要。先看 Introduction 和 Background，把 Muon 的更新机制重新过一遍，尤其是 polar(M)=UV^T 这一点。只有先接受“Muon 只在乎方向，不在乎奇异值幅度”，后面的设计才会显得自然。然后重点读方法部分的三小节。读 §3.1 时不要只看归一化公式，要看作者如何论证误差累积会形成 preferred direction；读 §3.2 时重点看结构分解后为什么误差主要落在奇异值而不是奇异向量；读 §3.3 时看 companding 为什么比均匀量化更适合 Muon 的状态分布。\n\n实验部分建议按三层看。第一层看主结果表：4-bit MuonQ 与 full-precision Muon 的差距到底有多小，和 naive 4-bit 的差距有多大。第二层看训练曲线与稳定性图，判断它是“最终能追上”还是“全程都稳”。第三层看消融和误差分析，确认 directional fidelity 不是事后解释，而是被实验直接支持的机制。若时间有限，至少看主结果、ablation、以及展示奇异向量/方向相似度的图。\n\n这篇还有几个值得深挖的点。其一，MuonQ 的思想是否能推广到其他带矩阵结构更新的优化器，或者推广到 Shampoo/Adafactor 一类低秩或结构化状态。其二，方向保真是否能转化成更直接的训练目标或误差界，而不只是设计原则。其三，top-k 结构分解在超大模型和分布式训练里会带来多少额外通信与算子开销，这决定它的真实系统收益。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a specific failure mode in low-bit training with Muon: why Muon is much harder to quantize to pure 4-bit than Adam-like optimizers, and how to do so without losing training quality. The key issue is not ordinary numerical reconstruction error. Muon updates are based on the polar factor of the momentum matrix, which discards singular-value magnitudes and keeps only directional information through the singular vectors. As a result, even small quantization errors can become harmful if they rotate those directions. The paper therefore reframes the objective from numerical fidelity to directional fidelity.","importance":"This is a meaningful training-side paper for pretraining researchers. First, it targets optimizer-state memory, which is a hard systems constraint in large-scale training, and it does so for Muon rather than for standard Adam states. Muon is attractive because of its convergence behavior and matrix-structured updates, but its practical value drops if optimizer states cannot be compressed aggressively. Second, the paper makes a broader methodological point: optimizer-state quantization should preserve the information the optimizer actually uses, not just minimize element-wise error. For Adam, magnitude statistics matter a lot; for Muon, directional structure matters more. That lesson is likely to generalize beyond Muon. Third, the work ties together optimizer mechanics, quantization error geometry, and training stability in a fairly coherent way, which makes it more useful than a purely empirical compression trick.","technical_approach":"$22","experiments":"$23","reading_guide":"$24","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"应读方法和稳定性实验；若内部在试 Muon，方向误差而非数值误差这个 prior 需要立刻做 ablation。","en":"Read the method and stability experiments; if you are testing Muon internally, the direction-error rather than value-error prior deserves an immediate ablation."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of California, Santa Barbara"],"authors":["Yupeng Su","Ruijie Zhang","Ziyue Liu","Yequan Zhao","Zheng Zhang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Muon","optimizer-quantization","llm-training","low-bit-training","training-stability"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11396","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.11396","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.11255","title":"HEBATRON: A Hebrew-Specialized Open-Weight Mixture-of-Experts Language Model","abstract":"We present Hebatron, a Hebrew-specialized open-weight large language model built on the NVIDIA Nemotron-3 sparse Mixture-of-Experts architecture. However, post-training DLMs with standard Negative Evidence Lower Bound (NELBO)-based supervised fine-tuning remains inefficient: training reconstructs randomly masked tokens in a single step, whereas inference follows a confidence-guided, multi-step easy-to-hard denoising trajectory. Recent trajectory-based self-d","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T09:39:06+00:00","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2605_11854.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作解决 diffusion language model 后训练中的训练—推理不一致问题：训练时用 NELBO 做随机 mask 的单步重建，推理时却沿着置信度驱动的多步 easy-to-hard 去噪轨迹逐步解码。已有自蒸馏轨迹方法多用于压缩采样步数，能改善解码效率，但不一定提升完整 diffusion 解码下的模型能力，甚至可能带来性能下降。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发在于，它把 DLM 的后训练目标从“拟合答案 token”推进到“拟合推理时的熵结构”。这对非 AR 语言模型很关键：DLM 的能力瓶颈不只来自数据或模型规模，也来自训练目标是否能塑造正确的去噪顺序和不确定性地形。它不是新的基座预训练范式，但对 DLM 的 SFT、轨迹蒸馏、RL 替代目标设计有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法名为 TABOM，即 Trajectory-Aligned Optimization via Boltzmann Modeling。核心做法是先用基座 DLM 生成自蒸馏去噪轨迹，再把推理时的 unmask 分布建模为关于 token 理想预测熵的 Boltzmann 分布；低熵 token 应更早被确定，高熵 token 应更晚被处理。直接最小化全局 KL 难以操作，因此作者用 pairwise ranking surrogate 近似优化，要求轨迹中 token 的局部熵排序符合 easy-to-hard 解码顺序。相比普通 NELBO，这个目标不只是让模型重建被 mask token，而是让模型的熵梯度与实际解码过程一致。","experiments":"实验基于 Dream DLM，在代码生成和数学推理上比较 ground-truth SFT 与 self-distilled trajectory SFT。代码数据从 Ling-Coder-SFT 随机采样 17K queries，数学数据来自 MixChain-Z-PRM12K；用 entropy-based decoding 生成有效自蒸馏轨迹，代码约 3.8K 条，数学约 5.1K 条。经验观察是，自蒸馏数据相对离线 GT 数据有更低优化障碍，但直接用标准 NELBO 微调只带来有限收益。TABOM 的实验主张是能把自蒸馏轨迹转化为更稳定的性能提升，同时保留 OOD 能力，缓解标准 SFT 常见的遗忘。作者还提出 Trajectory Discrimination Score，用来量化模型是否保留轨迹中的 token 级不确定性差异；Figure 2 和 Table 6 用于说明 TABOM 改变的是熵景观，而不是简单复用生成样本。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 30–45 分钟。先看 Introduction 中对 NELBO、DLM 推理轨迹、RL 不适配性的分析，这是问题定义的核心。然后看 empirical observations 部分，重点理解 self-distilled data 为什么优化更容易、为什么 naive NELBO 仍然不够。技术部分优先读 Section 3.1 的 Boltzmann 建模和 Section 3.2 的 pairwise ranking 目标；实验部分看 Section 4.1 的性能与遗忘结果，以及 Figure 2、Table 6 对 TDS 的分析。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The work targets a concrete training-inference mismatch in diffusion language models. Standard NELBO-based SFT trains the model to reconstruct randomly masked tokens in one step, while inference follows a multi-step confidence-guided denoising path that reveals tokens from easy to hard.","importance":"Its main value for pretraining-oriented research is the objective-level view of DLM post-training. The paper argues that improving a DLM is not only about adding supervised targets or compressing sampling steps; the training loss should also shape the entropy ordering used at inference time. This is relevant to non-autoregressive language modeling, trajectory distillation, and possible alternatives to rollout-heavy RL for DLMs.","technical_approach":"The proposed method, TABOM, uses self-distilled denoising trajectories generated by the base DLM. It models the inference-time unmasking distribution as a Boltzmann distribution over ideal token-level predictive entropy, where easier low-entropy tokens should be selected earlier. Since the resulting global KL objective is not tractable in practice, the method uses a pairwise ranking surrogate that enforces local entropy order along the trajectory. This differs from ordinary NELBO fine-tuning because the target is not just token reconstruction, but alignment between the model’s uncertainty landscape and its actual decoding schedule.","experiments":"The experiments are conducted on the Dream diffusion language model for code generation and mathematical reasoning. The code setting samples 17K queries from Ling-Coder-SFT, and the math setting uses MixChain-Z-PRM12K. Entropy-based decoding yields about 3.8K valid self-distilled trajectories for code and about 5.1K for math. The paper reports that self-distilled trajectories have a lower optimization barrier than offline ground-truth data, but naive NELBO training gives only marginal gains. TABOM is reported to improve performance while preserving out-of-distribution capability and reducing forgetting. The Trajectory Discrimination Score is introduced to measure whether token-level uncertainty gaps along the denoising path are preserved; Figure 2 and Table 6 are the key references for this claim.","reading_guide":"A focused skim should take around 30–45 minutes. This avoids materializing |V|-dimensional one-hot states and keeps the input dimensionality at the embedding size d. The method also introduces an S-arch backbone whose activations live on the hypersphere, matching the geometry of the input representation. computer-use agent 在长轨迹场景里的一个很具体但长期被低估的问题：历史截图确实有用，但现有 MLLM 把每一帧都当成独立图像编码，导致大量重复视觉 token 挤占上下文，结果不是“历史没用”，而是“历史表示太低效”。论文要解决的核心不是单纯压缩图像，而是在不改底层 MLLM 架构的前提下，按时间维度识别连续 GUI 截图中的冗余 patch，只保留真正变化或任务相关的区域，让模型能在同样上下文预算下看到更长的交互历史，并且学会基于这种稀疏历史做决策。","importance":"对基座研究的直接启发不在于 agent 应用本身，而在于它把“跨时间冗余建模”明确成了多模态序列建模里的训练问题，而不是推理时的工程裁剪。这个视角有两点价值。第一，它说明多图像/视频式上下文里，token budget 的主要浪费常常来自重复观测，而不是模型容量不足；如果训练时就暴露给模型压缩后的时序输入，性能和效率可以同时改善。第二，它给统一多模态预训练一个可迁移的思路：不是一味追求更长上下文，而是先问哪些 token 在时间上是可删的、删掉后模型是否还能维持空间结构和决策能力。对 GUI agent 是 patch redundancy，对视频、桌面操作、甚至语音离散 token 序列，也都对应“相邻帧/相邻片段高度重复”的同类问题。贡献量级上，这更像一个扎实的训练范式改造，而不是新的基础架构。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 ReVision Token Selection（RTS）加上基于过滤轨迹的端到端微调。先说冗余定义：作者把相邻两帧截图按同一视觉 tokenizer 的 patch 网格对齐，对每个位置的 patch embedding 做比较，判断当前帧该 patch 是否可由前一帧“解释”，如果可以，就视为冗余。为此他们训练了一个轻量三层 MLP 分类器，输入是相邻截图中对应 patch 的表示，输出该 patch 是否应保留。这里的关键设计不是做全局检索式压缩，而是保留原有二维空间布局，只在 patch 级别做删减，这样可以继续兼容 Qwen2.5-VL 这类现成视觉-语言架构，不需要改 attention 结构或重写位置编码。\n\n训练流程分两步。第一步训练 RTS，让它学会在时间上识别冗余 patch。第二步把 RTS 接入 MLLM 数据流水线，对 AgentNet 轨迹中的历史截图做 patch 过滤，再用这些压缩后的多步视觉历史去微调 Qwen2.5-VL-7B。论文强调这不是推理时外挂式 token pruning，而是让模型在训练阶段就适应“部分历史图像只保留变化区域”的输入分布，因此模型会学会把证据分散地整合到多帧里，而不是默认每一帧都完整可见。\n\n与常见 baselines 的区别主要有三点。其一，不是简单减少历史帧数，而是在固定帧数下减少每帧冗余 token，因此保留了更长时间跨度。其二，不是基于启发式像素差分，而是用 learned selector 在 embedding 空间判断冗余，鲁棒性更高，也更接近模型实际感知空间。其三，不改 backbone，因此结论更干净：收益主要来自时序冗余消除和训练分布匹配，而不是额外模型容量。","experiments":"实验覆盖 OSWorld、WebTailBench、AgentNetBench 三个长程 computer-use benchmark，底座模型是 Qwen2.5-VL-7B。论文先做了一个很重要的统计：连续 GUI 截图之间平均有 45.4% 的 patch 不发生变化，某些设置超过 56%，折算下来平均每一步有 1000 多个冗余 patch。这为方法提供了明确动机。\n\n主结果上，在使用 5 张历史截图时，ReVision 平均减少约 46% 的视觉 token，同时相对不做 drop 的 baseline 带来约 +3% 的 success rate 提升。若只给 3 张历史图，ReVision 的表现已经接近一些更重的 baseline，但视觉 token 量大约只有一半。随着历史长度增加到 5 张及以上，ReVision 相对同规模 baseline 的优势更稳定，平均至少高 2% 左右。论文最有信息量的观察不是单点提升，而是历史长度-性能曲线的形状变化：在普通表示下，性能较早饱和；做了冗余过滤后，继续增加历史仍能带来收益。这说明过去很多“history 不再有用”的结论，部分是被低效视觉表示误导的。\n\n从消融角度，值得注意的点通常包括： learned RTS 相比不加选择或简单保留最近帧更优；训练时使用过滤后的轨迹优于只在推理时做过滤；在相近 token 预算下，保留更多时间步但每步更稀疏，优于保留更少时间步但每步完整。这些结果共同支持作者的中心论点：长程 GUI 决策更缺的是有效历史，而不是原始像素堆叠。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但建议把几个关键位置看仔细。先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1，核心结论已经很清楚：问题不是历史无效，而是视觉 token 冗余导致历史装不进去。然后看 Temporal Visual Redundancy 和 Table 1，这部分决定了方法是否值得做；如果你关心多模态预训练里的 token economy，这一节最有启发。方法部分重点看 Figure 2 和 RTS 的输入输出定义，确认它如何在不改 backbone 的情况下保留空间结构。实验部分优先看不同 history length 下的曲线，而不是只看单个 benchmark 平均分，因为真正有价值的是“延后饱和点”这个现象。\n\n预计 20 到 30 分钟可以抓住主要信息。如果要深挖，建议追问三件事：一，RTS 的监督信号具体如何构造，是否会把语义上重要但视觉变化小的区域误删；二，patch 过滤后位置编码和视觉聚合是否存在分布偏移；三，这种时间冗余压缩能否推广到原生视频预训练或多帧 VLM，而不只是 GUI agent 微调。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific bottleneck in computer-use agents: history is useful, but screenshot history is represented in a wasteful way. Standard MLLMs encode every GUI frame independently, so long trajectories quickly fill the context window with repeated visual tokens. The real issue is not that past observations stop helping, but that the model spends too much budget re-encoding unchanged interface regions. ReVision addresses this by learning to remove temporally redundant patches across consecutive screenshots while preserving the spatial structure needed by the underlying vision-language model.","importance":"Its value for foundation-model research is less about the agent setting itself and more about the framing: temporal redundancy reduction is treated as a training-time modeling problem, not just an inference-time compression trick. That matters because it suggests a broader principle for multimodal pretraining: before scaling context length, ask which tokens are redundant across time and whether the model can be trained directly on a compact temporal representation. The paper also provides evidence that apparent history saturation in GUI agents is partly an artifact of inefficient visual tokenization. That idea should transfer to other sequential modalities with high local redundancy, including video and discrete audio streams.","technical_approach":"$28","experiments":"$29","reading_guide":"$2a","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"适合做 VLM agent 的 token-budget baseline；重点看 selector 是否保空间结构，46% token 降幅比 3% 成功率更有用。","en":"Good baseline for VLM-agent token budgeting; inspect the selector and spatial preservation, since the 46% token cut matters more than the 3% gain."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of British Columbia","Microsoft Research"],"authors":["Amirhossein Abaskohi","Yuhang He","Peter West","Giuseppe Carenini","Pranit Chawla","Vibhav Vineet"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["computer-use-agent","visual-token-reduction","VLM-efficiency","GUI-agent","long-context"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11212","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.11212","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.11317","title":"SOMA: Efficient Multi-turn LLM Serving via Small Language Model","abstract":"Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in multi-turn dialogue settings where preserving conversational context across turns is essential. A standard serving practice concatenates the full dialogue history at every turn, which reliably maintains coherence but incurs substantial cost in latency, memory, and API expenditure, especially when queries are routed to large proprietary models. efficie","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-11T23:07:33+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_11317.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"多轮对话线上服务通常每一轮都要把全量历史拼回去给大模型，导致重复计算、延迟和成本随轮次线性上升；但直接把后续轮次交给小模型又会因早期上下文“定锚”而逐步漂移。论文要解决的是：在不每轮调用大模型、且不牺牲多轮一致性的前提下，让小模型在单个会话的“局部行为流形”上逼近大模型。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接影响有限，更偏系统/Serving 与会话内自适应蒸馏。价值点在于两件事：1）把“会话内分布是局部的”明确成可操作的建模假设（local manifold approximation），提供了一种 session-level 的轻量对齐思路；2）给出一个经验观察：多轮对话 token 分布呈长尾，早期轮次更长且信息密度更高，后期轮次更短但强依赖早期锚点——这对做长上下文训练数据配比、对话压缩/记忆模块的评估设计有启发。","technical_approach":"SOMA（Soft-prompts for lOcal Manifold Approximation）是一个三阶段会话内适配框架，目标是用少量大模型调用，把小模型“拉到”当前会话诱导的局部区域。\n1）Soft prompt tuning：在会话早期轮次上，为小模型学习软提示，刻意寻找能放大“小模型 vs 大模型”语义行为差异的方向（文中表述为最大化语义发散/behavioral divergence）。直觉是：先找到最容易出错、最能暴露差距的局部切向方向，后续对齐更高效。\n2）Localized fine-tuning：用少量输入-输出对（由大模型在早期轮次产生/标注）对小模型做局部微调（文中提到 localized LoRA），让小模型在该会话的局部流形上模仿大模型。这里的关键取舍是“会话内局部拟合”而不是“全局蒸馏”：牺牲跨会话泛化，换取单会话的快速贴合与成本下降。\n3）Efficient inference：推理阶段尽量做到 prompt-free（软提示/LoRA 已内化），并结合 extractive summary 来降低输入长度；同时用一个基于余弦相似度的 gate 做模型切换，并配 rollback 机制监测潜在 topic shift，一旦偏离局部区域就回退到大模型以保质量。\n整体上它不是在压缩 KV-cache 或做 attention 复用，而是把“后续轮次的生成”转化为“会话内小模型对大模型的局部模仿问题”。","experiments":"给定的全文片段未包含实验设置与具体数字（模型规模、数据集、成本节省比例、质量指标、消融等），因此无法基于全文复述关键结果。可从论文的实验部分重点核对：\n- 质量：与全程大模型相比的胜率/偏好评测、以及多轮一致性指标（是否随轮次漂移）。\n- serving commonly resends the full dialogue history to a large model at every turn, causing redundant compute, latency, and cost growth with conversation length. Simply handing later turns to a small model degrades coherence because early turns anchor the session and later turns depend heavily on that context. The paper targets session-level efficiency: adapt a small model to approximate a large model’s behavior within the local region induced by the early turns, so later turns can be served cheaply without losing consistency.","importance":"This is primarily a serving/system paper rather than a pretraining-method contribution. Its relevance to foundation-model researchers is indirect: (1) it operationalizes a “session-local distribution/manifold” assumption and turns it into a lightweight, per-session alignment recipe; (2) it highlights an empirical long-tail pattern in turn-level token counts—early turns are long and information-dense, later turns are shorter yet more context-dependent—which can inform how we design dialogue pretraining mixes and how we evaluate memory/compression mechanisms beyond naive truncation.","technical_approach":"$2b","experiments":"The provided full-text excerpt does not include the experimental section or any concrete numbers (model sizes, datasets, cost/latency reductions, win rates, ablations). Yet every token generated during the decode phase triggers thousands of kernel launches, and kernel launch overhead alone can account for 14.6% of end-to-end inference time. MegaKernel eliminates launch overhead and inter-operator HBM round-trips by fusing multiple operators into a single persistent kernel. However, existing M","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T06:04:28+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_11581.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在毫秒级强时延约束的线上场景（广告系统）里，LLM decode 阶段存在大量 kernel launch 与算子间 HBM 往返，导致端到端时延被 launch overhead 和内存流量主导；MegaKernel 虽能通过算子融合与 persistent kernel 缓解，但在 NVIDIA Ada（L20）这类共享内存更小、缺少原生 TMA 的 GPU 上，很难同时做到“足够快、可移植、且无运行时动态调度抖动”。","importance":"这是推理系统/编译优化论文，不是预训练方法论文；对基座预训练的直接贡献有限。但它把一个常被忽略的约束讲得很具体：在真实线上 1–5ms SLA 下，任何运行时分支、动态调度、以及过度依赖新架构特性的优化都会变成不稳定因素。对做 pretrain 的启发主要在工程闭环：模型结构选择（如 attention/MLP 形态、KV cache 访问模式）会反过来决定能否做深度融合与稳定低延迟服务，从而影响“可训练—可部署”的整体最优。","technical_approach":"Ada-MK 的核心是把 MegaKernel 在 Ada 上落地，并把“动态决策”尽量前移到离线：\n1) 自适应共享内存管理：把 shared memory 需求建模为硬件规格×模型结构×动态 workload 的函数，通过 K 维度细粒度切分（K-dimension splitting）降低峰值 shared memory（文中声称可降 50%），并做跨算子 page 复用，以在 128KB/SM 的约束下重建可用的 producer-consumer pipeline。\n2) DAG 级自动搜索：用 MLIR lowering 构建到 PTX 级的依赖 DAG，在离线 profiling 中固化“最优执行 trace”，运行时直接按序执行，避免 Mirage MPK 那类 managed pointer/页状态 if-else 带来的指令发射效率下降与尾延迟抖动。这里的关键取舍是：牺牲部分在线自适应，换取确定性与更低的控制开销。\n3) 异构混合推理引擎：作为 TensorRT-LLM plugin 集成，Prefill 继续用 TensorRT-LLM 原生算子（更通用、对长序列/大 batch 友好），Decode 切到 MegaKernel（更低 launch 与更少 HBM round-trip）。这相当于承认“prefill/decode 最优实现不同”，用系统层切换来拿到两边的收益。","experiments":"文中给出的关键量化点包括：在 Qwen2.5-1.5B + TensorRT-LLM 的 profile 中，kernel launch overhead 约占端到端 14.6%（约 1,655,550 次 launch，累计约 3.3s 的 launch 时间，具体测量口径来自 Nsight Systems）。在多种 Qwen 系列模型上，相比 vLLM / SGLang / vanilla TensorRT-LLM，端到端时延降低 10%–50%，并已在百度商业广告系统上线。全文片段未包含更细的表格数字（如具体 batch/seq、prefill vs decode 拆分、p50/p99、吞吐-时延曲线、以及与 Hopper 上 MegaKernel 的对照），这些需要在实验章节核对。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 25–40 分钟）：\n- 先看 Introduction 的约束设定与 profile 数字，确认它解决的是“确定性低时延”而非纯吞吐。\n- kernel 的对比，判断“离线固化”是否牺牲了泛化到更多形状的能力。","authors_note":"百度（商业广告系统/推理工程团队）；具体作者信息在给定全文片段中未出现。"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Under strict 1–5ms SLA in production (online ads), LLM decoding suffers from massive kernel-launch overhead and inter-op HBM round-trips. MegaKernel-style fusion can remove much of this overhead, but deploying it on NVIDIA Ada (e.g., L20)—with smaller shared memory and no native TMA—faces a portability–efficiency–determinism trade-off: hand-tuned kernels are brittle, while auto-compiled approaches often introduce runtime branching/scheduling that hurts ultra-low-latency stability.","importance":"This is an inference/compilation systems paper rather than a pretraining-method paper, so its direct impact on foundation-model pretraining is limited. The useful takeaway for pretraining researchers is the “train–serve closure”: architectural choices (operator shapes, KV-cache access patterns, attention/MLP layouts) constrain how far operator fusion and persistent-kernel execution can go, and in real SLA-bound deployments, runtime dynamism (branches, adaptive scheduling) can be worse than a slightly suboptimal but deterministic plan.","technical_approach":"$2c","experiments":"Reported highlights include: profiling Qwen2.5-1.5B under TensorRT-LLM shows kernel-launch overhead is ~14.6% of end-to-end time (~1,655,550 launches, ~3.3s cumulative launch time). Across Qwen model variants, Ada-MK reduces end-to-end latency by 10%–50% versus vLLM, SGLang, and vanilla TensorRT-LLM, and is deployed in Baidu’s production ads system. st","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T08:23:00+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_11744.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文处理的是长上下文建模里一个常被忽略但很关键的问题：很多方法在训练时仍用 full-context attention，而在推理时为了省算力改成分段、窗口化或受限上下文执行。这样做虽然推理便宜，但训练和推理的执行语义不一致，模型在训练时学会依赖的信息，在真实推理时并不存在，导致长上下文泛化和稳定性受损。作者要解决的核心问题不是单纯做更便宜的 long-context inference，而是把“受限分段执行”提升为训练和推理共享的建模假设，让模型从一开始就在与部署一致的状态转移规则下学习。","importance":"对基座研究来说，这篇工作的意义在于它把长上下文问题从“注意力算子优化”转回到“训练目标与执行语义是否一致”。这和预训练里很多问题是同构的：比如 teacher forcing 与 test-time rollout 的偏差、full attention 训练配 sparse attention 推理的偏差、训练时可见全局信息而部署时只能看局部状态的偏差。作者给出的框架说明，若把跨段信息流限制到一个受控接口状态，并把更远程的信息访问改成前向只读，那么 TBPTT 不再只是近似梯度，而是在该受限语义下的精确梯度。这一点很重要，因为它把“截断反传会不会伤目标”这个问题重新定义了：如果目标本身就是 inference-consistent 的分段目标，那么截断就是正确训练，而不是权宜近似。对做长上下文预训练、状态压缩、外部记忆、streaming LM 的人，这个视角有直接参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法的核心是把序列切成若干不重叠 segment，并把跨 segment 的信息流拆成两条通道。第一条是可微的局部连续性通道 C_i，也就是固定大小的 carried KV tail。它是唯一允许在相邻 segment 间递推的状态接口，训练和推理都按同样方式传递。第二条是前向只读的远程条件通道 R_i，即 retrieved KV prefix，用来提供超出 carried-KV 视野的长程证据，但它不参与状态递推，也不允许梯度穿过历史链条。\n\n在这个设计下，每个 segment 的前向算子在训练和推理时完全相同：当前 segment 的输出由当前 token、上一个 segment 传来的 C_{i-1}，以及远程检索得到的 R_{i-1} 共同决定。关键约束是，只有 C 构成递归状态链，R 只是额外条件。训练时作者使用 TBPTT，但不是对 full-attention 目标做近似，而是把跨段 credit assignment 明确限制到最近 K 次状态转移。由于目标本身就是在这个受限递归上定义的，所以 TBPTT 在这里计算的是 inference-consistent objective 的精确梯度。论文还强调，在实验中 K=1 已经足够且最优，意味着只需要对最近一次状态传递反传即可。\n\n架构实现上，作者采用 head- 和 layer-sparse 的 long-range heads：少数头负责长程检索或远程条件，大多数头负责局部、可携带状态的计算。这样做的动机很清楚：如果所有头都承担长程依赖，状态接口会膨胀，训练和推理的一致性也更难维持；把长程能力限制在稀疏子结构里，更容易控制计算和语义边界。论文的设计取舍也很明确：它不追求保留 full attention 的精确语义，而是主动定义一种受限但一致的执行语义，用一致性换取可扩展性和部署可靠性。","experiments":"从引言和方法部分能确认的关键结果有三类。第一，作者在多个 long-context benchmark 和不同上下文长度下验证了该框架在受限执行条件下仍有较强表现，说明训练-推理一致的分段建模并没有因为限制状态传播而明显失效。第二，关于反传长度的消融显示 TBPTT 的 K=1 已经 sufficient and optimal，这个结果很关键，因为它意味着跨段学习所需的可微信号窗口可以非常短，训练成本和实现复杂度都能显著下降。第三，在 128K context prefill 场景下，相比 full attention，内存占用约降低 6×。这说明它不仅是理论上更一致，也确实带来可观的系统收益。\n\n不过，真正需要在正文里核实的实验细节包括：segment 长度如何设定；retrieved KV prefix 的来源与检索策略；长程头占比多少；在 perplexity、needle-in-a-haystack、长文问答、长程复制或多跳检索等不同任务上，和 full-attention train / segmented inference 的 baseline 差距有多大；以及一致性训练是否改善了推理阶段的稳定性而不仅是平均分数。如果这些结果完整，论文的说服力会明显增强。","reading_guide":"先读 Method 开头和 Figure 2，弄清楚 C 和 R 两条通道分别承担什么角色。这里最容易误读的一点是：R 不是 memory state，而是 forward-only conditioning；真正的递归状态只有 carried KV。接着看训练目标的形式化部分，重点确认作者为何说 TBPTT 在这里是 exact gradient，而不是常见意义上的近似。这个论证是全文最有信息量的地方。然后看架构实现，关注 long-range heads 的稀疏设计，因为这决定了方法到底是一个理论框架，还是可落地的 Transformer 变体。最后看 ablation，尤其是 K=1 最优、不同 segment size、是否需要 Many practical systems train with full-context attention but switch to segmented, windowed, or otherwise bounded execution only at inference time to save memory and compute. That creates a mismatch in both execution semantics and state evolution: the model learns during training to rely on information that will not be available under the constrained inference regime. The paper is not mainly about making inference cheaper in isolation. It is about making segmented execution the shared modeling assumption for both training and inference, so the model is optimized under the same state-transition rules that it will face at deployment.","importance":"For foundation-model research, the key value is the shift in perspective. This is closely related to several broader issues in pretraining: teacher-forcing versus rollout mismatch, full-attention training with sparse-attention inference, and any setting where training exposes global information that deployment cannot use. The strongest conceptual point is their treatment of TBPTT. If the objective itself is defined under a bounded, inference-consistent segment recurrence, then truncated backpropagation is no longer merely an approximation to a larger full-context objective; it is the exact gradient of the objective the model is actually meant to solve. At each step, the model processes the next chunk conditioned on the accumulated cache, appends the newly produced keys and values, and passes the enlarged cache forward; the same one-step update is applied repeatedly, analogous to foldl in functional programming. Building on the KV cache concatenation primitive introduced for l","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T17:53:47+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_12471.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的是一个很具体的推理问题：不改模型、不再训练，能不能把标准 decoder-only Transformer 的 KV cache 直接当作跨 chunk 的递归状态，用分块方式处理超长上下文，同时尽量保留对早期 token 的精确访问能力。它针对的是现有长上下文方案里的一个老矛盾：全量注意力在超长序列上算不动，流式窗口和压缩缓存又会丢信息，尤其不适合需要精确回忆远距离细节的场景。","importance":"对基座预训练本身的直接贡献有限，因为它不改变训练目标、模型结构或数据配方；但它对理解 Transformer 的“可递归性”有启发。文章最有价值的点不是提出了一个更省显存的工程技巧，而是给出一个经验事实：把 KV cache 当作 chunk-to-chunk 状态传递后，误差并不会随链深持续累积，而是很快进入稳定平台。这说明预训练得到的注意力表征在一定程度上天然支持这种外部递归式使用。对做长上下文预训练、记忆机制、segment recurrence、甚至 latent state reuse 的研究者，这个观察值得记住。","technical_approach":"方法本身很简单。把长序列切成若干 chunk，处理第 t 个 chunk 时，不是只看本 chunk 内 token，而是把前面所有 chunk 产生的 KV cache 作为前缀直接拼接进当前注意力可见的历史中。当前 chunk 计算出新的 K/V 后，再把它们追加到累计 cache，传给下一个 chunk。作者把这个过程类比为函数式编程里的 left fold：KV cache 是 accumulator，每一步都执行同一个 one-step update。关键点有三个。第一，它完全训练无关，不引入 memory token，不改位置编码，不做近似注意力。第二，它保留了历史 token 的未压缩表示，因此远距离检索不是靠摘要向量，而是仍然依赖内容寻址。第三，它和常见 streaming 方法的取舍不同：streaming 追求常数级缓存，KV-Fold 接受线性增长的 cache，以换取更完整的历史可访问性。文中还专门分析了与 full-context forward 的差异来源。由于 chunk 边界改变了注意力计算的组织方式，结果不会与单次整段前向完全一致；但这种偏移表现为进入一个轻微偏移但稳定的 attention regime，而不是数值误差层层放大。","experiments":"实验重点放在稳定性、检索能力和资源开销。稳定性方面，作者观察到 per-step drift 在前几个 chunk transition 上升后很快进入平台，之后即使链很深也基本不再恶化；而且这个现象对 chunk size、模型家族、数值精度都比较稳，文中强调即便精度变化达到 10,000 倍量级，平台行为仍然存在。检索方面，needle-in-a-haystack 测试覆盖 16K、32K、64K、96K、128K 上下文，在 Llama-3.1-8B 的 128K 原生窗口内，最深链深达到 511 时仍能保持 100% 的插入事实召回。资源方面，作者给了一个很直观的对比：T=128K 时，单次 full-attention forward 在 40GB A100 上不可行，仅 attention score matrix 就接近 1TB；KV-Fold 的峰值显存约 35.6GB，171 秒可完成。与 StreamingLLM 比，KV-Fold 的峰值内存更高：类似设置下 StreamingLLM 维持 1024-token cache，峰值约 16.6GB，而 KV-Fold 的 cache 增长到约 17.18GB，并且总工作显存更高，但换来的是在所有测试距离上维持 100% 检索，而流式方法会随距离下降。文章的核心实验结论不是“更快”，而是“在可承受资源下保留精确远距访问”。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看引言和方法图，理解它与 streaming、cache compression、segment recurrence 的边界。然后重点看稳定性实验：作者关于 drift plateau 的图和对应讨论，是整篇文章最有信息量的部分，因为它回答了为什么这种简单递归没有迅速崩掉。接着看 needle-in-a-haystack 和内存/时延对比表，明确它适合什么场景、不适合什么场景。预计 20 到 30 分钟可以抓住主要内容。如果时间更少，只看方法定义、drift 曲线和 128K 检索结果即可。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a narrow but useful question: can a pretrained decoder-only Transformer reuse its KV cache as a recurrent state across chunks, so that very long sequences can be processed incrementally without retraining or architectural changes, while still preserving exact access to early tokens? The target pain point is the usual trade-off in long-context inference: full attention is infeasible at scale, while streaming windows and cache compression lose information that matters for exact recall.","importance":"Its direct relevance to pretraining is limited because nothing is changed in the training objective, architecture, or data recipe. The value is more diagnostic than algorithmic. The main takeaway is that chunk-wise KV recurrence does not show runaway error accumulation with depth; instead, the model quickly enters a stable shifted regime. That is an interesting empirical statement about pretrained Transformers: their internal representations appear more reusable as external recurrent state than standard single-pass usage would suggest. This matters for researchers thinking about segment recurrence, memory-augmented pretraining, latent-state reuse, and long-context training objectives.","technical_approach":"$30","experiments":"$31","reading_guide":"For a quick read, start with the introduction and the method figure to place KV-Fold relative to streaming, cache compression, and recurrent-memory approaches. Simply expanding the context window is costly and often fails to ensure effective context utilization. 层的耦合位置。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks how to equip a frozen LLM with an online, dynamically updated memory that can preserve useful history over long interactions without simply extending the explicit context window.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it is relevant to memory-augmented language modeling and test-time adaptation. The interesting part is the interface choice: instead of storing memory as text or using a separate retrieval channel, it maintains a tiny associative state and injects it as a low-rank correction inside attention. That makes it a useful reference point for future work on native memory modules that could eventually be co-trained with the backbone.","technical_approach":"δ-mem maintains an online state of associative memory, a matrix S, alongside a frozen full-attention backbone. As tokens arrive, the model derives memory keys and values and updates S with a delta-rule. The update writes only the residual error along the current key direction, so well-predicted associations induce little change while new or corrected information is written efficiently. A forget gate is added to stabilize long-range state evolution and balance retention against overwrite.\n\nAt read time, the method does not reconstruct text memory or perform external retrieval. The current input queries S to obtain an associative signal relevant to the present context, and that signal is transformed into a low-rank correction to the backbone’s attention components. In effect, memory steering happens inside the attention pathway rather than through prompt injection. The resulting module is compact, online, and compatible with a frozen backbone.","experiments":"The evaluation covers memory-heavy benchmarks such as HotpotQA, LoCoMo, and MemoryAgentBench, plus general capability benchmarks including IFEval and GPQA-Diamond. The headline result is that with only a fixed 8×8 online memory state, δ-mem improves the final average score by 1.10× over the frozen backbone and by 1.15× over the strongest non-δ-mem memory baseline. Gains are larger on memory-intensive tasks: over 1.31× on MemoryAgentBench, over 1.20× on LoCoMo, and the TTL subtask rises from 26.14 to 50.50. The main takeaway is less about raw benchmark leadership and more about how much useful history can be retained in an extremely small online state.","reading_guide":"For a quick read, start with the taxonomy in the introduction: textual memory, outside-channel memory, and parametric memory. This limits per-step parallelism, reduces information gain per iteration, and slows down overall inference speed.","importance":"This finding overturns the conventional assumption in dLLM parallel decoding: high confidence is not a necessary condition for safe token decoding. Generate a superposed context containing potential future information; 2. Compare predictions from the current context and the superposed context to identify medium-confidence tokens with low sensitivity to future updates and stable predictions; 3. Decode these tokens in advance to improve per-step parallelism and information gain without accuracy loss. Recently, GPU accelerators have added first-class support for microscaling Block Floating Point (BFP) formats. Standard BFP algorithms use a fixed scale based on the maximum magnitude of the block. We observe that this scale choice can be suboptimal with respect to quantization errors. In this work, we propose ScaleSearch, a","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T17:50:08+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_12464.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AR","cs.PF"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有块浮点（BFP）量化默认采用块内绝对值最大值推导缩放系数，未利用微缩放格式缩放系数的尾数精度做优化，导致量化误差高于理论最优值。","importance":"提出了无需修改硬件的低比特量化优化方案，覆盖权重、激活、KV缓存全链路FP4量化，对大模型推理效率提升和低精度预训练的缩放策略设计有参考价值。","technical_approach":"1. 提出ScaleSearch算法，遍历微缩放格式（如NVFP4）缩放系数可表示的相邻取值，选择使块内量化均方误差最小的缩放系数，而非直接使用最大值推导的缩放系数；2. 扩展得到ScaleSearchAttention，将该策略用于注意力Q/K/V张量、中间注意力矩阵的NVFP4量化，结合不相干处理、矩阵分解降低离群值影响，加入注意力sink感知的混合精度KV缓存；3. 所有运算可直接在张量核心上执行，无反量化开销。","experiments":"训练后权重量化场景下，ScaleSearch使Qwen3-8B的MATH500精度最多提升15个点；扩散模型Mochi的VQA任务中，FP4注意力精度比SageAttention3高14个点；ScaleSearchAttention将Llama 3.1 70B的困惑度从3.4降至2.6348，Llama 3.1 8B Instruct的GPQA Diamond精度提升5个点；量化额外开销为1.74倍，注意力吞吐量达到SageAttention3的98.3%。","reading_guide":"泛读重点看ScaleSearch的核心逻辑、ScaleSearchAttention的全链路FP4注意力设计，以及主实验的精度与吞吐权衡结果，预计阅读时间15分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Existing Block Floating Point (BFP) quantization defaults to block maximum absolute value for scale calculation, failing to leverage the mantissa precision of scales in microscaling formats, leading to higher than theoretically optimal quantization error.","importance":"Proposes a low-bit quantization optimization scheme that requires no hardware modification, covers full-stack FP4 quantization for weights, activations, and KV cache, and provides reference for LLM inference efficiency improvement and low-precision pretraining scale strategy design.","technical_approach":"1. Post-training quantization (PTQ) is a leading approach for compressing LLMs. Popular weight quantization procedures, including GPTQ and RTN, suffer in model utility, especially at aggressive quantization levels (sub-4-bit). We propose ADMM-Q, a novel weight quantization algorithm that considers the layer-wise quantization problem. Our algorithm is based on a combinatorial varian","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-11T20:33:41+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_11222.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 LLM 后训练量化里一个很具体但长期存在的问题：层级 weight quantization 的求解器不够好。RTN 基本忽略 Hessian 结构，只做独立舍入；GPTQ 虽然引入 Hessian，但采用按列贪心更新，误差会沿处理顺序累积，对列顺序和条件数都比较敏感，尤其在 W3 这类激进低比特下更明显。论文把层级量化重新写成 Hessian 加权的离散约束优化问题，目标不是改量化格式，而是换一个更稳、更全局的求解器。","importance":"对基座研究的直接意义不在预训练算法本身，而在训练后部署链路：同样的模型、同样的量化格式、同样的推理框架，求解器不同，低比特可用性会明显不同。这个点对大模型研究很实际，因为很多时候瓶颈不是有没有 3-bit/4-bit 方案，而是这些方案在真实模型族上是否稳定、是否能作为通用 PTQ 组件复用。ADMM-Q 的价值在于把“量化是启发式工程”往“量化是可分析的约束优化”推进了一步，并且能作为 GPTQ/RTN 的替换件接入现有 pipeline。对做 pretrain 的人，启发主要有两点：一是 Hessian 结构仍然是压缩里最有信息量的局部几何；二是 outlier/病态条件数问题会同时影响训练和压缩，两边的数值处理思路是相通的。","technical_approach":"方法核心是把单层量化写成最小化 Hessian 加权距离的约束问题，然后用 ADMM 做变量分裂。连续变量负责在实数域里优化重构误差，离散变量负责投影到量化集合，二者通过增广拉格朗日项逐步拉近。相比 GPTQ 的列级贪心补偿，ADMM-Q 是联合更新整层权重，因此不依赖列顺序，也不把早期量化误差硬性传递给后续列。\n\n实现上有三块比较关键。第一块是 diagonal scaling。作者指出 LLM 层输入激活存在明显 channel outlier，导致 X^T X 的条件数很差，直接做 ADMM 会不稳定。于是先按通道范数做缩放，把 Hessian 预条件化，再在缩放空间里优化。这个设计和 SmoothQuant 一类“把异常值搬运掉”的思路有相通之处，但这里服务的是求解器稳定性，不是直接改量化分布。第二块是 adaptive penalty schedule。ADMM 里的罚项如果过大，会过早把解拉向离散集合，连续优化空间不够；过小则离散约束收敛慢。论文采用逐步增大的 penalty，让前期更像 Hessian-aware 的连续拟合，后期再强化量化可行性。第三块是 local search refinement。在 ADMM 得到一个离散解后，再做局部离散搜索，修正一些单点投影造成的次优格点选择。这一步很像在 combinatorial neighborhood 里做后处理，弥补 ADMM 在非凸离散集合上的近似误差。\n\n论文还给了一个理论点：把已有针对 pruning 约束的 ADMM 收敛分析扩展到 quantization 约束。这个结果不是在说全局最优可得，而是说明在给定设置下，算法迭代到某类稳定点是可论证的。对工程方法来说，这比纯启发式更扎实，但阅读时不必把它理解成离散量化问题已被“解决”。","experiments":"从引言和结果描述看，实验覆盖了 weight-only 和 weight-activation 两条线，并且主要在 Qwen3 模型族上验证。结论比较明确：ADMM-Q 在 W-only 场景下持续优于 GPTQ，收益在 W3 这种 sub-4-bit 设定最明显；接入 SpinQuant、SmoothQuant 这类已有 W/A pipeline 后，也能继续带来提升，说明它不是依赖某个特定前处理技巧。部署侧，作者在 vLLM 中验证了与 GPTQ 相同的吞吐和显存收益，也就是精度提升基本不以推理效率为代价。\n\n从全文可提炼的实验重点有三类。第一类是主结果：perplexity 和 zero-shot accuracy 相对 GPTQ 的改进，尤其看 3-bit 和 4-bit 区间，因为这里最能体现求解器差异。第二类是组件消融：去掉 diagonal scaling、adaptive penalty、local search 后性能如何下降，这能帮助判断收益主要来自数值稳定、优化路径还是离散后处理。第三类是兼容性实验：在不同量化 pipeline、不同 bit-width、不同模型尺寸上是否都成立。如果阅读时只能抓重点，优先看 W3/W4 的主表和 ablation 表。\n\n当前给出的全文片段没有完整表格数字，因此不适合编造具体提升幅度。能确认的硬信息是：在 vLLM 部署下，Qwen-32B 的 W8A8 约有 1.5x 推理加速，且 ADMM-Q 保持与 GPTQ 相同的部署收益；性能提升主要体现在 perplexity 和 zero-shot accuracy，而不是吞吐。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合泛读，不必逐行推公式。建议先看 Introduction，明确它不是提出新量化格式，而是在同一目标函数下换求解器。然后直接看方法部分里三件事：Hessian-weighted formulation、diagonal scaling、adaptive penalty/local search。这里决定了它和 GPTQ 的真正差别。接着看主结果表，重点盯 W3/W4；如果这些低比特下收益不稳定，方法价值会打折。最后看 ablation，判断到底是哪一块在起主要作用。\n\n预计 25 到 40 分钟可以完成一轮有效阅读。若只关心是否值得纳入工具链，优先看：1）与 GPTQ 的 apples-to-apples 对比是否严格；2）是否需要额外校准数据或更高求解成本；3）在不同 pipeline 中是否真是 drop-in replacement。若关心研究延展性，可以深挖它的 ADMM 目标是否还能扩展到 activation quantization、KV-cache quantization，或者与 rotation/scaling 预处理联合优化。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific but important weakness in LLM post-training quantization: the layer-wise weight quantizer is often the limiting factor. RTN ignores curvature and rounds weights independently. GPTQ uses Hessian information, but solves the problem greedily column by column, which makes it order-sensitive and prone to error accumulation, especially in aggressive regimes such as 3-bit quantization. The paper reformulates layer-wise quantization as a Hessian-weighted discrete optimization problem and focuses on a better solver rather than a new quantization format.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model work is mostly on the deployment side, not on pretraining itself. Still, it matters because low-bit usability often depends less on the nominal bit-width and more on whether the solver remains stable across real model families. ADMM-Q is useful as a general PTQ component: same model, same quantization grid, same serving stack, but a stronger optimization procedure. The broader lesson is that local geometry, captured here by the Hessian, still carries most of the useful signal for compression, and that conditioning/outlier issues show up in both training and compression. MLLM隐藏态与输入嵌入范数差距的定量分析，这是整个工作的核心诊断依据；第3节GAP范式的三个层级设计细节，尤其是PCA对齐的具体实现与难度感知分配的采样策略；第4节的消融实验部分，可明确各组件的实际贡献量级；附录中token级执行路径的示意图，可直观理解隐式重建与文本解码的交互过程。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Existing visual latent reasoning methods for MLLMs universally adopt an \"output-as-input\" latent token feedback paradigm, which causes severe feature space mismatch on pre-norm MLLM backbones: the norm of decoder output hidden states in pre-norm Transformers is much larger than that of input embeddings the model was trained to consume (text hidden states have ~546× larger norm than text input embeddings, vision hidden states have ~8.7× larger norm than vision input embeddings). This mismatch makes latent token feedback unreliable, leading to unstable performance gains or even ineffective latent channels.","importance":"This work reveals the core failure mechanism of latent feedback on pre-norm MLLMs, providing an optimization direction of underlying feature compatibility for all output-reuse-based latent reasoning methods. The proposed three-level granular alignment paradigm achieves stable visual reasoning improvement via lightweight adaptation without modifying the backbone architecture or large-scale retraining. The open 49K latent supervision dataset also provides foundational data support for subsequent visual latent reasoning research.","technical_approach":"GAP (Granular Alignment Paradigm) covers three alignment levels across data, feature, and model dimensions: 1) Feature level: A PCA-aligned latent head maps RMS-normalized decoder hidden states into the principal subspace of auxiliary image vision embeddings, resolving feature distribution mismatch while enabling low-rank parameterization of the latent head. 2) Data level: Context-grounded latent supervision annotates each latent sample with chain-of-thought intent, a <latent> span supervised by auxiliary image embeddings, and a <parser> description recording the meaning of the latent signal, improving interpretability of latent tokens. 3) Model level: Difficulty-aware latent assignment estimates base model accuracy via repeated sampling and assigns latent supervision targets only to samples that the base model fails to solve, eliminating supervision noise from easy examples.","experiments":"Training-free intervention experiments on Monet-7B (a state-of-the-art latent model based on Qwen2.5-VL-7B) show that EMA norm scaling of re-injected latent tokens alone delivers a +2.00 performance improvement on MathVista. GAP outperforms all existing visual latent baselines on aggregate metrics across five visual perception and reasoning benchmarks, achieving the best average performance among supervised variants in the experiments. Ablation studies verify that PCA alignment, curated latent supervision, and difficulty-aware assignment each contribute independently to performance gains. Inference-time intervention experiments further confirm that generated latent tokens carry task-relevant effective visual signals, rather than merely adding token placeholders.","reading_guide":"Focus on Section 2 for quantitative analysis of the norm gap between pre-norm MLLM hidden states and input embeddings, which is the core diagnostic basis of the work. Read Section 3 for details of the three-level GAP design, especially the implementation of PCA alignment and sampling strategy for difficulty-aware assignment. Section 4 ablation studies clarify the actual contribution magnitude of each component. However, while using imagined trajectories reduces the sample complexity of policy training, existing methods still heavily rely on task-specific data to fine-tune both the world and reward models, fundamentally limiting their scalability to unseen tasks. To overcome this, we argue that world a","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T16:16:15+00:00","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2605_12334.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是一般意义上的机器人策略优化，而是一个更具体、也更卡脖子的问题：如何在目标任务完全未参与世界模型训练的前提下，用冻结的 world model 和冻结的 reward source，对新任务做高数据效率的 VLA 后训练。现有基于 imagined rollout 的 VLA-RL 方法虽然减少了真实交互，但 world model 往往仍要用目标任务数据重训或微调，reward 也常依赖任务专用分类器、参考轨迹或人工设计信号，所以一旦任务变了，整套流程都要重新收集数据。RAW-Dream 把这个依赖拆开：world model 只学任务无关的物理动态，reward 由通用 VLM 对 imagined video + instruction 做零样本判定，目标任务只需要少量 demonstration 把 policy 对齐到任务语义，再在梦境里做 RL。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义不在于它提出了一个新的 backbone，而在于它把“可复用的 task-agnostic simulator”明确成了一个预训练对象，并给出了一条比 task-specific world-model RL 更干净的扩展路线。这里有三个层面的价值。第一，它把 world model 的训练目标从“服务某个任务”改成“覆盖 embodiment 的物理先验”，这和基座模型预训练里追求 transferable prior 的思路是一致的。第二，它把 reward 从任务内监督中解耦出来，改用冻结 VLM 做 zero-shot success judgment，这相当于把通用视觉语义能力接到了 RL 后训练接口上。第三，它说明在 VLA 场景里，预训练和后训练的边界可以重新划分：world model 与 reward 准备一次，后续只适配 policy。这个范式如果成立，影响的是数据预算结构，而不只是单点指标提升。","technical_approach":"方法上有三块。第一块是 task-agnostic action-conditioned video world model。作者基于 WAN 2.1 搭建动作条件视频生成器，但训练数据严格来自 broad-coverage、与下游任务无关的交互数据，例如 play data 或 benchmark 中不含目标任务的任务集合。关键点不是模型结构新，而是训练约束新：world model 不允许看 target-task rollout，因此它必须靠更广覆盖的行为分布去学可迁移的物理动力学，而不是记住任务模板。\n\n第二块是 zero-shot reward。作者不训练任务专用 reward model，而是把 imagined rollout 视频和文本指令交给冻结的 Qwen3-VL，由其直接判断任务是否成功，输出二值奖励。这样 reward source 也不再依赖目标任务标注器。这里的取舍很清楚：牺牲一部分 reward 精细度，换取跨任务可复用性和零额外标注。\n\n第三块是在 world model 内做 policy optimization。策略采用 OpenVLA-OFT，先用极少量 demonstration 做 SFT，把策略锚定到新任务语义；然后在冻结 world model 中用 GRPO 做 RL。由于 imagined rollout 在未见任务上更容易出现 hallucinated success，作者加了 dual-noise verification：对同一初始条件，用不同 diffusion noise 生成多条 rollout，只有当 VLM reward 在不同噪声下保持一致时，才把该奖励视为可信。这一步不是简单 ensemble，而是专门针对生成式 world model 的 reward hacking 问题：如果成功只在某个噪声样本里出现，往往说明是模型幻觉而非稳定可实现的轨迹。\n\n从方法定位看，RAW-Dream 的创新不在单个组件，而在组合方式和约束设计。它没有重新发明 VLA、VLM 或 video diffusion WM，而是把三者放进一个严格 task-agnostic 的后训练闭环里，并补上了 hallucination-aware reward verification 这个必要环节。","experiments":"实验分成仿真和真实机器人两部分。仿真上，作者在 LIBERO 上训练 world model 时只使用 LIBERO-90 数据，并把 Spatial、Object、Goal、Long 四个 held-out task suites 作为 world-model 训练时完全未见的下游任务。这个设定比常见的 imagined RL 更严格，因为不是同任务低样本，而是 world model 对目标任务零暴露。结果上，RAW-Dream 相比 1-shot SFT baseline 带来 +8.9% 的提升，并且优于需要 50× 更多真实世界数据的 online RL。这个对比很关键，它说明 task-agnostic WM + zero-shot reward 并不是只在“省数据但性能差一些”的角落里成立，而是在数据效率上有明显优势。\n\n真实机器人上，world model 只用多样、未清洗的 play data 预训练，不看目标任务数据。每个新任务从 3-shot SFT 起步，再进入 imagined RL。最终成功率相对 3-shot SFT baseline 有 +21.7% 的绝对提升。对机器人场景来说，这个幅度已经足以说明 dream-based RL 不是纯粹的模拟器内自娱自乐，而是能把少量 demonstration 放大成可观的真实收益。\n\n从文中叙述看，值得注意的消融主要有三类：一是验证 task-agnostic WM 相比 target-task-specific 方案在 unseen-task setting 下的适配能力；二是验证 VLM zero-shot reward 的可用性；三是 dual-noise verification 对抑制 hallucinated success 和 false-positive reward hacking 的作用。虽然给出的摘要片段没有完整表格数字，但论文主结论依赖的不是单一主表，而是这三类消融共同支撑：world model 要足够通用，reward 要足够稳，RL 才能在未见任务上真正工作。","reading_guide":"精读时先抓三处。第一是 Introduction 里对问题设定的限定：不是一般的 world-model RL，而是“target-task-agnostic VLA-RL optimization in WMs”。这句话决定了整篇工作的评价标准。第二是 Method 里 world model、zero-shot VLM reward、GRPO、dual-noise verification 之间的接口设计，尤其要看 reward 是如何从 imagined video 中提取、如何做一致性过滤，以及 policy 更新时哪些模块冻结、哪些模块可训练。第三是实验部分关于 LIBERO held-out suites 和 real-robot play-data 预训练的设定，这些设定决定了结果是否真的说明了 transferable physical prior，而不是数据泄漏。\n\n如果时间有限，优先看：任务设定图、整体 pipeline 图、LIBERO 主结果表、dual-noise verification 消融、真实机器人结果。若要深挖，建议重点想两个问题：一，task-agnostic world model 的能力边界到底由数据覆盖决定，还是由生成模型 fidelity 决定；二，VLM reward 在 imagined video 上的误判会不会形成系统性偏差，dual-noise 只是降噪还是已经足够构成可靠的 reward filter。阅读时间大约 45–70 分钟，若连附录中的实现细节和失败案例一起看，可能需要更久。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a specific bottleneck in world-model-based VLA post-training: how to improve a policy on new tasks when the world model has never seen target-task rollouts and the reward source is also task-agnostic. Prior imagined-RL pipelines reduce real-world interaction cost, but they still depend on target-task data to train or adapt the world model, and often require task-specific reward classifiers or reference trajectories. That makes them hard to scale to unseen tasks. RAW-Dream reframes the setup: pretrain a reusable action-conditioned world model on broad interaction data, use a frozen VLM as a zero-shot reward judge over imagined videos plus instructions, and adapt only the policy with a small amount of demonstrations followed by RL inside the frozen simulator.","importance":"Its main contribution is not a new backbone but a cleaner decomposition of what should be pretrained once versus adapted per task. The world model is treated as a reusable physical prior rather than a task-bound simulator, and the reward is outsourced to a general VLM instead of a task-specific learned head. For pretraining-oriented readers, this matters because it shifts the scaling question from 'how much target-task data do I need to build a simulator' to 'how broad and transferable can my embodiment-level physical prior become.' It also gives a concrete pretrain-to-posttrain interface: prepare a task-agnostic simulator and reward mechanism once, then spend downstream data only on policy anchoring. That is a meaningful change in data allocation, not just a small algorithmic tweak.","technical_approach":"$35","experiments":"$36","reading_guide":"$37","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看方法图和实验表；若结果扎实，它会改变 VLA 后训练里“每任务重训 world/reward”的默认做法。","en":"Skim the method figure and main tables; if the results hold, it weakens the default of retraining world and reward models per VLA task."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Microsoft Research Asia","Nanjing University","University of Illinois at Urbana-Champaign","Wuhan University","University of Technology Sydney","Tsinghua University"],"authors":["Yucen Wang","Rui Yu","Fengming Zhang","Junjie Lu","Xinyao Qin","Tianxiang Zhang","Kaixin Wang","Li Zhao"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["VLA","world-model","RL-post-training","VLM-reward","robotics"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12334","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.12334","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.11405","title":"20/20 Vision Language Models: A Prescription for Better VLMs through Data Curation Alone","abstract":"Data curation has shifted the quality-compute frontier for language-model and contrastive image-text pretraining, but its role for vision-language models (VLMs) is far less established. We ask how far data curation alone can take VLM performance, holding architecture, training recipe, and compute fixed and varying only the training data. O","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T01:51:03+00:00","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2605_11405.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在 VLM 预训练里，“只改数据、不改模型/训练配方/算力”到底能把能力推到哪里、能否系统性提升而不是只在少数任务上变好。论文把数据当作一等设计变量，要求在严格 matched-compute 条件下给出可复现的增益，并回答：哪些数据处理环节（去重、过滤、配比、合成）对哪些能力轴最关键，以及这些增益是否稳健（OOD、seed、上下文长度、开放式交互）。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接贡献是把 VLM 的“数据策展”从零散技巧拉到可度量、可组合、可对照的工程闭环：在 1B/2B/4B 三个尺度上，单靠数据就能把 cost-quality Pareto 明显外推，并在很大程度上缩小与“更大算力+重度 post-train（SFT/RL）”公开模型的差距。更关键的是它展示了数据策展不仅提升均值，还显著降低 seed 方差、改善 OOD 与开放式行为（更具体、更诚实、拒答更少），这对后续做 scaling law、数据配比、以及 pretrain↔posttrain 接口设计都有直接参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法是一个端到端的数据策展流水线，核心点在“联合（joint）”与“目标分布匹配（target-distribution matching）”。\n1) 联合去重：不是只做文本去重或图像去重，而是做跨模态的 joint image-and-text dedup，减少同图多文、同文多图、以及模板化网页带来的重复信号，目标是降低有效 token/样本的冗余，提高梯度多样性。\n2) 联合过滤：同时对图像与文本做质量过滤，而不是只用 caption 质量或只用图像美学分数。过滤目标更偏“训练信号可学习性”：可读 OCR、描述与图像一致性、噪声/水印/拼贴、低信息量 caption 等。\n3) 配比设计：用 target-distribution matching 让训练混合更贴近评测/真实使用分布（论文把能力拆成多个轴：OCR/文档、图表、数学、计数、空间/3D、品牌识别、caption、VQA、grounding、多图推理等），避免单一 web 图文对把模型推向“泛描述”而缺少结构化与符号密集能力。\n4) 合成数据：同时使用 task-agnostic 与 task-specific synthetic data。前者更像“补足长尾视觉语言现象”的通用增强（例如更清晰的描述、更规范的 OCR 表达），后者更像“对齐某些能力轴的监督形状”（例如图表/数学/计数/品牌等更可控的样式）。论文的主张是：单点改进（只去重/只过滤/只合成）容易把增益局限在局部能力；把这些环节组合起来，才能在 20 个公开 benchmark 与 DatBench 的多轴评测上形成一致提升。\n5) 严格对照：架构固定为 Qwen3 LM（1B/2B/4B）+ SigLIP2 视觉编码器，训练配方与算力固定；baseline 用同一来源的未策展数据（MAmmoTH-VL-12M 的 single-image 10M 子集），只替换数据版本。并对 baseline 与 curated 都做同等的多模态去污染（decontamination），避免“数据更干净所以更像测试集”的伪增益。","experiments":"核心结果围绕“同算力、只改数据”的增益与稳健性：\n- 20 个公开 VLM benchmark 平均提升：1B +13.0pp，2B +11.7pp，4B +14.0pp（论文强调每个尺度都 Pareto-dominant）。\n- DatBench 九个能力轴平均提升：2B +11.3pp，并且随规模增大增益更明显（说明策展后的数据更能被更大模型利用，符合数据质量提升后 scaling 更顺的直觉）。\n- OOD：9-eval OOD 平均在 2B 上 +7.2pp。并且出现“训练中缺失能力也提升”的现象：例如 BLINK 的 multi-image reasoning 总体 +3.09pp，其中 Visual_Correspondence +11.8pp（尽管它需要跨图对应关系）。这更像是表征与对齐质量提升带来的迁移，而不是记忆。\n- 稳定性：跨 seed 的按能力统计标准差从 2.47pp 降到 0.82pp（约 67% 降幅），说明策展不仅提高均值，也减少训练不确定性/坏种子。\n- 上下文长度：从 4k 到 16k 的 context sweep 下优势保持，暗示增益不是靠某个特定长度的“提示工程式”偶然。\n- 开放式交互：约 1100 条开放式查询（OCR、品牌/IP 识别、场景描述、拒答校准等）上，curated 2B 更具体、更诚实，且对 benign query 拒答更少；同时回答更简洁。论文把这作为“超出 benchmark 的行为质量”证据。\n- 与强参考模型对比：在最多约 150× 更少训练算力、且不做重度 post-training 的前提下，curated 模型在多项评测上逼近（或部分接近）同尺度的公开强模型（Qwen3-VL、InternVL3/3.5、Qwen3.5 等）。这里的关键点不是“超过谁”，而是把差距的主要来源之一明确指向数据，而非必须依赖 RL/SFT 才能获得的能力。","reading_guide":"精读建议按“可复用的方法论”来读：\n- 先看 Figure 1（cost-quality Pareto）与 §4 This interleaved design limits efficiency and keeps reasoning fragmented across separate text and vision channels. We propose UniVLR, a unified visual latent reasoning framework that treats textual reasoning and auxiliary visual evidence as a shared visual workspace. This keeps a separate text reasoning channel, but it limits efficiency and makes visual evidence weakly integrated: later text tokens often attend sparsely and diffusely to earlier auxiliary images.","importance":"The paper is relevant to VLM pretraining and native multimodal reasoning. Its useful idea is that textual reasoning does not have to remain as discrete text tokens during inference. It can be rendered as a visual object, spatially composed with crops, annotations, sketches, or diagrams, and compressed into visual latent tokens. This connects rendered-text understanding, visual tokenization, latent reasoning, and unified multimodal modeling.","technical_approach":"UniVLR uses a two-stage training pipeline. The first stage, Visual Latent Grounding, aligns autoregressively generated visual latent tokens with auxiliary images from visual CoT traces, so the model learns to produce semantically meaningful latent visual representations. The second stage, Text–Vision Unified Alignment, renders each textual reasoning step into an image and spatially concatenates it with the corresponding auxiliary image. The vision encoder representation of this unified image becomes the latent alignment target. At inference time, the model no longer emits explicit textual CoT. It generates only unified visual latent tokens and then decodes the final answer in natural language. The key difference from interleaved text-plus-visual-latent methods is that UniVLR removes the independent text reasoning path and moves the reasoning trace into the visual channel, relying on the vision encoder’s OCR and layout understanding to process both text and visual evidence together.","experiments":"The experiments cover V*, HRBench, and MME-RealWorld, with multiple base MLLMs including Qwen2.5-VL. The reported average gain over interleaved visual latent reasoning methods such as Monet, LVR, and SkiLa is 5.4% accuracy, while reducing the number of generated tokens by 15.2x. Attention analysis shows denser and more focused attention to auxiliary images under the unified visual representation. Hidden-state analysis also suggests more coherent latent-token distributions, indicating better alignment between textual reasoning content and auxiliary visual evidence in the shared latent space.","reading_guide":"Skim in 30–40 minutes. We identify the root cause as the mode-seeking nature of these methods, which maximize expected reward without effectively constraining probability distribution over acceptable trajectories, causing concentrat","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-09T04:41:02+00:00","category_id":"multimodal_gen","cover_url":"/covers/2605_10983.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有RL对齐扩散/流匹配生成模型的方法普遍存在奖励黑客问题，根源是标量奖励最大化目标本身的模式寻求特性，导致生成多样性下降、不可靠奖励被放大，出现视觉模式坍缩；现有缓解方案要么KL正则效果有限，要么GFlowNet类方法需要估计难以处理的状态流函数，引入额外训练误差。","importance":"从优化目标根源上解决扩散模型RL对齐的奖励黑客问题，轨迹级分布匹配的思路可以迁移到LLM的RL对齐以缓解模式坍缩问题，树采样的效率优化也可借鉴到多轨迹采样的训练效率提升场景。","technical_approach":"提出轨迹匹配策略优化（TMPO），将优化目标从标量奖励最大化改为轨迹级奖励分布匹配，核心设计包括：1）Softmax轨迹平衡（Softmax-TB）目标，对完整去噪轨迹做奖励分布匹配，将同一起点的K条轨迹的生成概率匹配到基于奖励的玻尔兹曼分布，组内归一化消除配分函数，支持直接优化；2）动态随机树采样，让轨迹共享去噪步骤，减少冗余计算，降低训练时间，同时促进不同去噪阶段的有效探索。","experiments":"在FLUX.1-dev模型上测试三个对齐任务：组合图像生成、视觉文本渲染、人类偏好对齐，平均生成多样性比现有领先方法提升9.1%，同时取得最优的GenEval准确率和PickScore，训练时间最多减少27%，在奖励-多样性、奖励-效率的权衡上表现最优。","reading_guide":"泛读建议：先看引言部分对扩散模型RL对齐奖励黑客根源的分析，方法部分的Softmax-TB目标推导和动态树采样设计，实验部分的多样性和效率对比结果，预计阅读时间15分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Existing RL alignment methods for diffusion and flow-matching generative models suffer from severe reward hacking, rooted in the mode-seeking nature of scalar reward maximization objectives, leading to reduced generative diversity, amplified unreliable rewards and visual mode collapse. Existing mitigation methods either have limited effect with KL regularization, or introduce extra training error by requiring intractable state-flow function estimation for GFlowNet-based approaches.","importance":"It addresses reward hacking in diffusion model RL alignment from the root of optimization objectives. The trajectory-level distribution matching idea can be migrated to LLM RL alignment to mitigate mode collapse, and the tree sampling efficiency optimization can be referenced for multi-trajectory sampling training efficiency improvement.","technical_approach":"Trajectory Matching Policy Optimization (TMPO) replaces scalar reward maximization with trajectory-level reward distribution matching. Core designs include: 1) Softmax Trajectory Balance (Softmax-TB) objective, which performs reward distribution matching on complete denoising trajectories, matching generation probabilities of K trajectories from the same starting point to a reward-based Boltzmann distribution, with in-group normalization to eliminate partition function for direct optimization; 2) Dynamic Stochastic Tree Sampling, which allows trajectories to share denoising steps to reduce redundant computation and training time, while promoting effective exploration across different denoising stages.","experiments":"Evaluated on FLUX.1-dev across three alignment tasks: compositional image generation, visual text rendering and human preference alignment. TMPO improves average generative diversity by 9.1% over existing leading methods, achieves the best GenEval accuracy and PickScore, reduces training time by up to 27%, and obtains the most favorable trade-offs on reward-diversity and reward-efficiency.","reading_guide":"Skim the analysis of the root cause of reward hacking in diffusion RL alignment in the Introduction section, the derivation of Softmax-TB objective and dynamic tree sampling design in the Methodology section, and the diversity and efficiency comparison results in the experiment section. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T08:47:05+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_11775.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究 token-level 的可观测信号。许多 RLVR 失败模式表现为 entropy collapse，但常见做法是加全局 entropy bonus、clip 或调 KL。这些方法知道“熵太低”，但不区分哪些 token 更新在压缩分布，哪些更新在扩展分布。论文提出 entropy polarity，把 advantage、sampled token 概率和当前分布形状联系起来，使熵控制从全局正则变成局部更新重加权。这个思路对推理模型训练、verifiable reward 数据构造、以及 RL 是否在扩大可探索空间都有参考意义。","technical_approach":"论文从 GRPO 出发。对于 prompt x，模型采样一组回答，每个回答得到 response-level reward，并用组内归一化 advantage 优化 clipped surrogate objective。作者把每个 token 的局部分布写成 softmax logits z，在状态 s_t 下采样 token y_t，分析由 A log π(y_t|s_t) 诱导的一阶 logit 扰动。softmax policy gradient 会提高 sampled token 的 logit，同时按当前概率降低其他 token 的相对位置。熵变化 ΔH_t 可以一阶分解为 sampled-token contribution 和 state-wise distributional correction。由此得到 entropy polarity：一个带符号的量，预测当前 token 更新倾向于增加还是减少局部 entropy，并估计幅度。核心观察是非对称性：强化高概率 token 更容易让分布变尖；想让熵上升，通常需要低概率 token 获得正 advantage，或者需要分布校正项足够强。基于这个信号，Papo 不再统一调节 entropy，而是区分 positive-polarity 和 negative-polarity 更新，根据训练中的 entropy trajectory 动态重分配 token-level advantage 权重。熵接近坍缩时提高扩展分布的更新权重；需要巩固 reward-aligned 行为时允许收缩分布的更新发挥作用。","experiments":"论文声称在数学推理和 agentic tool-calling 场景上验证 Papo，并与 RLVR/RFT baseline 比较，在 in-distribution 和 out-of-distribution benchmarks 上都有稳定收益。经验分析包含两个关键点：第一，entropy polarity Instead of treating entropy as a single training curve, it asks whether reinforcing a sampled token at a given decoding state will sharpen or flatten the next-token distribution, whether that direction can be predicted, and how it can be used to control exploration and exploitation.","importance":"The work is useful for understanding the pretrain-to-posttrain interface because it turns entropy into a local training signal. Many RLVR failures show up as entropy collapse, but common interventions such as entropy bonuses, clipping, or KL tuning operate globally and do not identify which token updates are entropy-expanding or entropy-contracting. Entropy polarity links the sampled token, its probability, the surrounding distribution, and the signed advantage. This makes entropy control more targeted: the optimizer can change the pressure on different token updates rather than pushing the whole policy with one scalar regularizer.","technical_approach":"$39","experiments":"The paper reports experiments on mathematical reasoning and agentic tool-calling, with comparisons against reinforcement fine-tuning baselines on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution benchmarks. Keeping only one branch is insufficient for stable RL training. Its main explanation is geometric: existing methods mostly change dominant representation components, while these components are also the easiest ones for later fine-tuning to recover. Minor components are changed less, but are harder to reverse once targeted.","importance":"The work is mainly about post-training unlearning, but it has a clear implication for pretraining and continual training research. A drop in loss or benchmark performance after unlearning does not necessarily mean the knowledge has been removed from hard-to-recover parts of the model. If the intervention lies in high-variance representation directions, ordinary fine-tuning can quickly undo it. This also informs safety-oriented continual pretraining: training signals tend to act first on dominant spectral directions, so apparent forgetting can be a reversible shift rather than durable removal.","technical_approach":"The paper reviews common unlearning methods such as Gradient Ascent, NPO, RMU, and MLP Breaking, then analyzes internal representations through their spectral structure. Representations are decomposed into dominant and minor components. The empirical and theoretical claim is that standard unlearning objectives preferentially modify dominant directions; relearning attacks also update these directions efficiently, which explains rapid recovery. Minor Component Unlearning, or MCU, instead applies the unlearning pressure to the minor-component subspace. The method differs from representation-misdirection approaches such as RMU because it does not merely push activations toward an arbitrary target; it chooses intervention directions according to the representation spectrum. The tradeoff is clear: MCU gives up some of the easiest optimization directions to obtain forgetting that is harder to reverse.","experiments":"The experiments cover WMDP-Cyber, WMDP-Bio, and Years. They evaluate immediate forgetting, recovery after relearning attacks, and utility on retained or unrelated tasks. The paper reports that existing unlearning methods can recover a large fraction of the forgotten knowledge under relearning, with cited evaluations showing recovery rates above 88%. MCU reduces post-attack recovery while preserving general utility. The experiments also support the mechanism: dominant components are strongly modified during unlearning but recover quickly during relearning, whereas minor components recover more slowly. The provided text does not include the full numerical tables, so exact per-dataset values should be checked in the experiment section and appendix.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 25 to 40 minutes. Focus first on the representation decomposition and theoretical analysis: how dominant and minor components are defined, where the projection is applied, and how recovery is measured. In the experiments, prioritize the before-and-after relearning tables on WMDP-Cyber, WMDP-Bio, and Years, plus the utility retention results. Readers interested in pretraining dynamics can mainly read the spectral analysis and recovery-dynamics plots. For tasks where the base policy cannot reach reward states, additional training or external guidance is needed to recover effective learning signals. Rather than relying on costly iterative supervised fine tuning (SFT), we exploit the abundant action data generated in everyday human agentic RL 的冷启动问题：当 base policy 的能力边界之外才有 reward state 时，纯在线 RL 根本采不到正样本，group-based advantage 退化成零，训练直接停住。作者关心的不是如何再堆一轮 SFT，而是能否利用大量现成的 action data，在不依赖显式 CoT 标注的情况下，把策略推过这个 reachability barrier，让 RL 重新获得有效学习信号。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对 post-train 尤其是 agentic RL 很有启发，因为它把“探索能力受 base model 限制”这个常见经验现象形式化了，并给出一种比 SFT warm start 更轻的替代路径。对基座研究员的价值主要有两点。第一，它提醒人们：后训练效果常常受限于 base model 的可达区域，而不是 reward 设计本身。第二，它展示了 action trajectory 这种弱于 full reasoning trace、但规模更大更自然的数据，如何作为中间训练信号使用。这对 tool-use、GUI/CLI agent、甚至部分合成数据管线都有参考意义。","technical_approach":"$3a","experiments":"实验集中在 search-agent benchmark，覆盖不同 base model、不同难度、以及 in-domain / out-of-domain 设置。主结论很直接：ActGuide-RL 在所有测试模型上都优于 zero RL，而且任务越难、原始 RL 越拿不到信号，收益越明显。\n\n文中给出的代表性数字基于 Qwen3-4B-Instruct：相对 zero RL，GAIA 提升 +10.68 个百分点，WebWalkerQA 提升 +27.79 个百分点，XBench 提升 +19.00 个百分点，BC-ZH 提升 +5.15 个百分点。这个幅度说明方法主要解决的是“从不会到会一点”的可达性问题，而不是在已可解任务上做小修小补。\n\n另一个重要结果是，它在没有 cold-start SFT 初始化的情况下，表现可以接近 SFT+RL pipeline。这一点很关键，因为作者的目标就是减少对 warm-start 数据和课程设计的依赖。如果这个结论在更多环境上成立，意味着 action-only data 可以在一定程度上替代显式监督冷启动。\n\n从消融上，最值得关注的是 guidance 强度与训练效果的关系。作者结论是“越多 guidance 越好”并不成立；最佳策略是把 guidance 当作零奖励 fallback，按 minimal intervention 原则使用。另一个应重点看的消融是 mixed-policy training 是否真的让 unguided policy 获益，而不是只在 guided evaluation 下变强。若文中有分开报告 guided / unguided success rate，这部分最能说明 internalization 是否成立。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，重点放在问题设定、方法直觉和关键消融，不必在形式化定义上停太久。\n\n建议先看引言和 Figure 1，理解作者说的 in-region / out-region 与 capability barrier。然后看 Method 里 reachability dynamics 的定义，抓住 effective state-visiting mass 和为什么 zero-reward 会导致 group RL 无梯度。接着直接看 action guidance 的实现、minimal intervention 机制、mixed-policy optimization。最后看主结果表和 guidance 强度相关消融。\n\n阅读时可以带着几个判断点。1）action guidance 是否只是把 demonstration 以另一种形式塞回模型，还是确实保留了 RL 的探索与 credit assignment 优势；2）这种方法对 action data 的质量、覆盖度、任务接口格式有多敏感；3）如果换成更强 base model，收益会不会迅速缩小，说明它主要是冷启动补丁而不是通用训练范式。\n\n预计 25–35 分钟可以读完主线。若你做 agentic states lie outside the reachable region of the current base policy, online RL cannot obtain positive trajectories, group-based advantages collapse, and learning stalls. The question is whether one can recover useful exploration without relying on an expensive SFT warm start. The proposed answer is to use abundant action data as guidance during RL, even when those data do not come with explicit reasoning traces.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it is relevant to the pretrain-to-posttrain interface. It also highlights a useful data type for post-training: action trajectories are weaker than full reasoning annotations, but they are cheaper, more natural, and often already available in tool-use or environment-construction pipelines. Existing approaches are limited in several ways: 1) off-policy methods such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and knowledge distillation (KD) suffer from exposure bias and limited recovery from model-generated errors over long horizons; 2) on-policy reinforcement learning methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) better align trainin","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T15:04:18+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_12227.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是长上下文 post-training 里的一个老问题：离线监督方法稳定，但一旦模型进入自己生成的轨迹，误差会沿着很长的生成链条累积；纯 on-policy RL 又能对齐真实 rollout 分布，但奖励稀疏、方差大、训练不稳，尤其在长上下文推理里更明显。作者要解决的不是单点算法改进，而是给出一个更完整的长上下文对齐配方：怎样把 on-policy 的分布匹配能力、token 级蒸馏的稠密监督、以及 reward-driven 优化结合起来，让模型在几千到上万 token 的推理和生成中既能保持正确性，也能保持结构和连贯性。","importance":"对预训练研究本身，它不是新基座架构或新预训练目标，但对 pretrain↔post-train 接口有直接价值。原因有两点。第一，长上下文能力并不只由 RoPE 外推或继续预训练决定，后训练阶段是否在模型自己的长轨迹上学习，影响很大。第二，这篇工作把“稀疏奖励 RL”和“稠密 teacher guidance”放到同一个 on-policy 框架里，给出了一个比单独 GRPO 或单独蒸馏更实用的折中。这个思路对 reasoning model、continual post-train、甚至后续 mid-training 都有参考意义：如果训练目标需要覆盖长 horizon，单靠离线 imitation 往往不够，单靠 RL 又太贵且不稳，混合信号可能是更现实的路线。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 dGRPO，名字上看是把 GRPO 和 on-policy distillation 叠加，真正关键在于三件事如何配合。第一，训练数据不是固定 teacher traces，而是当前 student policy 在长上下文任务上的 on-policy rollout，因此训练分布与推理分布一致，能减轻 exposure bias。第二，在这些 rollout 上同时施加两类学习信号：一类是 GRPO 风格的组相对优势信号，用于直接优化任务奖励；另一类是 token-level 的 teacher distillation，用 teacher 在相同 prefix 下的分布给出稠密监督，帮助模型在长轨迹中维持局部决策质量。第三，作者在实现上去掉了常见 RLHF 里的 reference KL，设 β_ref=0，不再显式约束到初始策略，理由是 reasoning/long-context 场景下策略自然偏离初始模型未必有害，而且省显存和算力。\n\n从方法定位看，SFT/KD 是 off-policy + dense signal，但没有 reward objective；GRPO 是 on-policy + reward objective，但 token 级监督稀疏；OPD 是 on-policy + dense signal，但不能直接优化任意 reward。dGRPO 试图同时拿到这三者的优点。文中还配套提出 LONG-BLOCKS 合成数据集，覆盖 multi-hop reasoning、contextual grounding、long-form generation，作用不是单纯扩充样本量，而是给长上下文 on-policy 对齐提供更丰富的任务分布。若从训练机制理解，dGRPO 的价值主要在 credit assignment：reward 负责全局方向，distillation 负责局部稳定，两者都发生在模型自己采样到的状态上，因此比离线 KD 更贴近真实误差传播链条。","experiments":"从全文可见，作者做的重点不是追求单一 benchmark 的高分，而是系统比较几种长上下文后训练策略：SFT/KD、OPD、GRPO、dGRPO。实验结论很明确：单独的离线方法在短程结构学习上有效，但随着上下文和输出长度增加，恢复模型自生成错误的能力不足；单独 GRPO 能提升 reward 对齐，但训练波动更大，样本效率更差；dGRPO 在长上下文任务上更稳定，且通常能同时保住短上下文能力。文中反复强调“preserving short-context capabilities of the starting model”，说明他们把能力保持当作主要评估维度之一，而不是只看长上下文分数。\n\n就可见内容而言，论文给出了方法比较表、训练 recipe 图，以及 LONG-BLOCKS 数据构成，但你提供的全文片段没有包含完整结果表、参数规模、训练 token 数、具体 benchmark 分数和提升幅度，因此没法负责任地填入精确数字。可以确认的是：实验设计围绕 Qwen3-based setup 展开，且比较对象覆盖 off-policy、on-policy RL、on-policy distillation 以及混合方法。若后续拿到完整附录，最该核对的是三类数字：一是不同 context length 下的性能曲线；二是训练稳定性指标，如 reward variance、loss/advantage 波动；三是短上下文通用能力是否出现回退，以及回退幅度。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Table 1 和方法总览图，先把四类训练范式的差别吃透：采样分布、学习信号稠密度、是否直接优化 reward、是否缓解 exposure bias。然后重点看方法章节里 dGRPO 的目标函数定义，确认蒸馏项和 GRPO 项是如何加权、在什么 rollout 上计算、teacher 是否只提供 logits 还是还参与筛选。接着看实验部分的主表和训练曲线，尤其关注三件事：长上下文收益是否随长度增加而扩大；dGRPO 相比纯 GRPO 的稳定性改善是否显著；短上下文能力保持是否真实而非口头描述。\n\n如果时间有限，预计 25–35 分钟可以抓住主线。若要深挖，建议额外看 LONG-BLOCKS 的数据生成细节，因为这会影响你对方法收益来源的判断：提升到底来自更好的算法，还是来自更合适的长上下文训练任务分布。另外，作者把 β_ref 设为 0，这个决定值得单独审视，尤其要看是否在某些任务上带来风格漂移或安全性退化。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a practical bottleneck in long-context post-training: off-policy methods such as SFT and KD are stable and dense, but they train on teacher trajectories rather than the model’s own long rollouts, so exposure bias compounds over long horizons; pure on-policy RL methods better match inference-time states, but become noisy, sparse, and sample-inefficient when rewards arrive late. The paper asks a recipe-level question rather than a narrow algorithmic one: how to combine on-policy learning, dense token supervision, and reward optimization so that an LLM can reason coherently over very long contexts without sacrificing its existing short-context behavior.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it matters for the pretrain-to-post-train interface. Long-context capability is not determined only by positional extrapolation or continued pretraining; the distribution on which the model is aligned after pretraining also matters. The paper is useful because it treats long-horizon alignment as a signal-design problem: sparse reward gives global direction, dense teacher guidance stabilizes local token decisions, and on-policy sampling makes both signals land on the states the model will actually visit. That framing is relevant to reasoning models, continual post-training, and possibly mid-training recipes where long-horizon behavior is a target.","technical_approach":"$3e","experiments":"$3f","reading_guide":"Start with Table 1 and the overview figure. After that, go straight to the main comparison tables and training curves. The most important questions are: does the advantage of dGRPO grow with longer contexts, does it materially stabilize training relative to pure GRPO, and does short-context performance remain intact rather than merely degrade less.\n\nFor a quick pass, 25 to 35 minutes should be enough. A design choice shared by nearly all such methods, however, has gone unquestioned: the teacher always sees the full reference reasoning. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T03:15:58+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_11458.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.LO"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文处理的是 on-policy self-distillation 中一个被默认忽略的设计点：teacher 在给学生 rollouts 做 token-level 监督时，是否应该总是看到完整参考推理。作者指出，full exposure 会造成 teacher-side exposure mismatch：teacher 条件中包含的推理信息超过学生当前能力，生成的软目标过强，学生难以吸收。","importance":"这篇论文对 reasoning post-training 更直接，但对预训练研究也有接口意义。它把“监督信号强度”从固定超参变成可学习控制变量，说明更强的 teacher context 不一定带来更好的学习信号。这个结论可以迁移到 curriculum、self-training、process supervision、RL 数据构造等场景：关键不是给模型最多信息，而是让目标分布落在当前模型可学习的范围内。","technical_approach":"方法叫 ATESD。基础框架是 OPSD：同一个模型同时作为 student 和 teacher。student 只看题目并生成 on-policy rollout；teacher 额外看参考解，对同一条 rollout 给出 token 分布；训练时只更新 student，最小化 teacher 到 student 的逐 token forward KL，并使用 KL clipping 稳定训练。论文的改动是把 teacher 可见的参考推理比例设为连续变量 α∈[0,1]，而不是固定为 1。作者先做固定 exposure sweep，发现 α=0.5 一类中等暴露比 full exposure 更稳；同时 teacher-student mismatch 随 α 单调增长。ATESD 用一个轻量 Beta-policy controller，根据训练状态统计量输出 α。controller 不是每步更新，而是在 hold window 内保持一个 exposure 决策；student 每个 distillation step 更新；controller 通过 REINFORCE 使用 discounted learning-progress reward 更新。这个 reward 评估 exposure 决策对后续若干步学生提升的影响，避免用单步 KD loss 下降来错误归因。","experiments":"论文在 AIME 2024、AIME 2025、HMMT 2025 上评估，模型包括 Qwen3-1.7B、Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B。固定 exposure 实验显示两个现象：full exposure 不是可靠最优；teacher-student mismatch 随 exposure 增大而单调上升。难度分桶分析显示，不同题目难度和训练阶段偏好的 exposure 不同，支持自适应控制。主结果中，ATESD 在这些数学推理 benchmark 上持续优于 self-distillation 与 RL baseline；Qwen3-4B 达到 65.65 Average@12。消融的核心信息是：连续 exposure 控制、hold/lookahead 两时间尺度、用未来学习进展而不是单步代理 reward，对效果都很关键。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 25–35 分钟。重点看三处：第一，§3.2 的 fixed-exposure sweep，那里给出 full exposure 失效和 mismatch 单调增长的证据；第二，方法部分关于 Beta controller、hold/lookahead schedule、discounted learning-progress reward 的定义，这是论文主要机制；第三，主实验和消融表，关注 ATESD 相比 OPSD、固定 α、RL baseline 的差距。若只关心可迁移启发，可重点读 exposure mismatch 的定义和 reward 设计，不必深读所有数学 benchmark 细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies an overlooked design choice in on-policy self-distillation for reasoning: whether the teacher should always condition on the full reference reasoning when supervising the student’s own rollouts. The authors identify a teacher-side exposure mismatch. When the teacher sees reasoning far beyond the student’s current capability, its token targets become too strong and less learnable.","importance":"The work is mainly about reasoning post-training, but the lesson is relevant to pretraining-adjacent training design. It treats the strength of the supervision signal as a controllable variable rather than a fixed assumption. More privileged context for the teacher does not necessarily produce better learning targets. The same issue can appear in curriculum learning, self-training, process supervision, RL data generation, and other settings where model-generated trajectories are trained against stronger conditional distributions.","technical_approach":"$40","experiments":"The experiments cover AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and HMMT 2025 with Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-4B, and Qwen3-8B. The fixed-exposure study reports two key patterns: full exposure is not a reliable optimum, and teacher-student mismatch increases as exposure grows. Difficulty-binned analysis indicates that different problem regimes prefer different exposure levels, motivating adaptive control. In the main results, ATESD consistently improves over self-distillation and RL baselines. On Qwen3-4B, it reaches 65.65 Average@12. The main ablation signal is that adaptive exposure, the two-timescale hold/lookahead schedule, and the future learning-progress reward are central to the gains.","reading_guide":"Skim time: about 25–35 minutes. Read the fixed-exposure sweep in §3.2 first, because it establishes why full teacher exposure is not a safe default. Then read the method section for the Beta controller, the hold/lookahead schedule, and the discounted learning-progress reward. Finally, inspect the main result and ablation tables to see how much of the gain comes from adaptive exposure versus the reward and scheduling choices. Readers mainly interested in transferable training insights can focus on the mismatch definition and credit-assignment design.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看 exposure sweep；它提醒我们别迷信更强 teacher，目标分布太远会把蒸馏信号做脏。","en":"Read the exposure sweep; it is a useful warning that a stronger teacher can make distillation targets less learnable."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Zihao Han","Tiangang Zhang","Huaibin Wang","Yilun Sun"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["self-distillation","reasoning","teacher-student","post-training","training-signal"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11458","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.11458","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.11609","title":"Anti-Self-Distillation for Reasoning RL via Pointwise Mutual Information","abstract":"On-policy self-distillation, where a student is pulled toward a copy of itself conditioned on privileged context (e.g., a verified solution or feedback), offers a promising direction for advancing reasoning capability without a stronger external teacher.
