会更确信那些已由答案暗示的结构词和可验证陈述，却降低对 Wait、Let、Maybe 等探索性推理 token 的支持。结果是蒸馏信号削弱了多步搜索所需的 deliberation。","importance":"这篇工作属于 post-train / RL reasoning 方法，但对预训练研究也有间接价值。它说明 token-level 训练信号的方向比“是否更有信息”更关键。带答案的上下文并不一定提供好的中间过程监督；它可能鼓励捷径式轨迹，而不是鼓励模型保留搜索空间。这个观察对 RL pretrain、过程监督、合成推理数据过滤都有借鉴意义：训练信号需要区分“答案已知后的解释”与“求解过程中的探索”。","technical_approach":"论文先用 pointwise mutual information 分析自蒸馏信号。teacher 分布为 πθ(· | x, c, y_<t)，其中 c 是训练时可见的 verified solution 或反馈；student 分布为 πθ(· | x, y_<t)。标准 self-distillation 最小化 student 到 teacher 的逐 token divergence，相当于奖励在 privileged context 下概率上升的 token。问题是，privileged context 会让 teacher 像 oracle 一样偏向已知答案所支持的 token，而不是偏向求解时有用的探索 token。Anti-Self-Distillation 的处理很直接：不下降 divergence，而是上升 student 与 teacher 之间的 Jensen-Shannon divergence，从而反转逐 token 信号。JSD 的形状给 advantage 提供天然边界，减少梯度过大风险；另外加入 entropy-triggered gate，当 teacher 的逐 token 熵塌缩时关闭该项，避免过度推开一个已过于确定的 teacher。方法可作为 GRPO 中逐 token 辅助项的替换，不需要外部 teacher 或单独的 process reward model。","experiments":"实验覆盖 5 个 4B–30B 模型，主要评测数学推理，也包含 coding 任务。数学基准包括 AIME 2024 和 AIME 2025。结果显示，默认 self-distillation 通常不能稳定超过强 GRPO baseline；AntiSD 能在 2–10 倍更少的训练 step 内达到 GRPO baseline 的准确率，并在最终准确率上最高提升 11.5 个百分点。诊断实验表明，默认蒸馏会增强结构连接词和答案相关声明的概率，同时压低 deliberation token；反向 JSD 后，这个逐 token 信号方向被翻转。消融中，JSD 的有界性和 entropy gate 是稳定训练的关键组件。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 30–40 分钟。先看 Section 3.1 的 conditional PMI 推导，这是理解方法动机的核心；再看 Section 3.2 中 AntiSD 如何从 divergence descent 改成 divergence ascent，以及为什么选 JSD 而不是普通 KL。实验部分重点看 Figure 1、Figure 2、Section 4.1 的模型规模 sweep，以及 entropy gate 和 divergence choice 的消融。若关注 RLVR 训练稳定性，可以细读 advantage 形状和 gate 触发条件。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses why on-policy self-distillation gives inconsistent gains for math reasoning. In the standard setup, the same model acts as a teacher under privileged context, such as a verified solution or feedback, and the student is trained to match that teacher token by token. The paper shows that this privileged teacher becomes confident on tokens already implied by the known solution, while assigning less support to deliberative tokens such as Wait, Let, and Maybe. This weakens the exploratory behavior needed for multi-step reasoning.","importance":"This is a post-training and reasoning-RL paper, but it has useful implications for pretraining-oriented researchers who care about training signals. It shows that richer conditioning is not automatically better supervision. A solution-conditioned teacher may produce a shortcut signal: it rewards tokens that make sense after the answer is known, not tokens that help discover the answer. The lesson transfers to RL pretraining, process supervision, and synthetic reasoning-data construction: token-level signals should distinguish retrospective explanation from search-time deliberation.","technical_approach":"The analysis starts from a pointwise mutual information view of self-distillation. The student distribution is πθ(· | x, y_<t), while the teacher distribution is πθ(· | x, c, y_<t), with c denoting privileged training-only context. Standard self-distillation minimizes a token-level divergence between them, so tokens whose probability increases under c receive positive pressure. In math reasoning, this favors solution-implied structure and verifiable claims, while suppressing tokens used for reconsideration and branching. Anti-Self-Distillation reverses this pressure by ascending a Jensen-Shannon divergence between student and teacher instead of descending it. The JSD form gives a bounded token-level advantage, and an entropy-triggered gate disables the term when the teacher distribution becomes too collapsed. The method can be inserted into GRPO as a replacement for the usual self-distillation auxiliary loss, without using an external teacher or a separate process reward model.","experiments":"The experiments cover five models from 4B to 30B parameters on math reasoning benchmarks, including AIME 2024 and AIME 2025, with additional coding evaluations. Default self-distillation often fails to beat a strong GRPO baseline. AntiSD reaches the GRPO baseline accuracy in 2x to 10x fewer training steps and improves final accuracy by up to 11.5 points. Token-level diagnostics show the intended sign change: the standard loss reinforces tokens implied by the privileged solution, while AntiSD pushes back against that shortcut signal. Ablations indicate that the bounded shape of JSD and the entropy gate are important for stable training.","reading_guide":"Skim in 30–40 minutes. Start with Section 3.1 for the conditional PMI identity, since it explains the failure mode rather than just proposing a new loss. Then read Section 3.2 for the divergence-ascent objective, the choice of JSD, and the entropy gate. In the experiments, focus on Figure 1, Figure 2, the model-size sweep in Section 4.1, and ablations on divergence choice and gating. Readers working on RLVR stability should pay special attention to the induced token-level advantage shape.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看的是它对 self-distill 失败机制的 token 级拆解，不是方法名本身；先看 PMI 分析和 entropy gate，若 setup 不干净就把结论当弱信号。","en":"The useful part is the token-level diagnosis of why self-distillation fails, not the method name; read the PMI analysis and entropy gate first, and treat the gains as weak evidence unless the setup is unusually clean."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Xiaohongshu Inc","Institute of Automation, Chinese Academy of Sciences"],"authors":["Guobin Shen","Xiang Cheng","Chenxiao Zhao","Lei Huang","Jindong Li","Dongcheng Zhao","Xing Yu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["reasoning-rl","self-distillation","PMI","chain-of-thought","post-training"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11609","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.11609","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.11853","title":"GEAR: Granularity-Adaptive Advantage Reweighting for LLM Agents via Self-Distillation","abstract":"Reinforcement learning has become a widely used post-training approach for LLM agents, where training commonly relies on outcome-level rewards that provide only coarse supervision. advantage。具体来说，论文用同一个模型构造 ground-truth-conditioned teacher，也就是在可见正确答案或正确条件的情况下生成参考分布；on-policy student 则按当前策略生成轨迹。二者之间的 reverse KL 被用来衡量学生在某个位置是否偏离了“更接近任务正确延续”的行为。KL 较大的位置通常对应推理路径发生转向或承诺的地方。论文进一步用 token entropy 作为边界信号，因为高熵位置常对应不确定性或语义转折，从而把单点 KL spike 扩展成更合理的行为片段。最后，GEAR 不引入显式 step reward，而是把 token-level aligned region 和 segment-level divergent region 转换成局部权重，对原始 trajectory-level advantage However, the core of these systems has not changed much since early instruction-tuned models like ChatGPT. Even advanced AI agents function on message exchange formats, successively exchanging messages with users, systems, with itself (i.e. chain-of-thought) and tools in a single stream of computation. This bottleneck to a","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T17:47:41+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_12460.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是传统意义上的语言建模精度问题，而是 chat-style LLM 的单流执行瓶颈：用户输入、模型输出、内部思考、工具交互都被线性串到一条 token 序列里，导致模型只能“读完再想、想完再写、写完再等”。这种串行化在长工具调用、持续交互、代码代理和计算机使用场景里会带来高首 token 延迟、难以边读边写、难以在生成中响应新信息，也让 system / user / model / thought 的边界在长上下文里变得模糊，进而影响 prompt injection 鲁棒性和可监控性。","importance":"对基座研究的启发主要有三点。第一，它把 instruction tuning 的对象从“单序列对话模板”改成“多路并行 token 流”，这是一种输入输出接口层面的建模改写，虽然不是新的 pretrain objective，但会影响后续 agent 形态、数据组织方式和推理系统设计。第二，它指出很多 agent 能力问题并不一定来自模型参数不足，而是来自 chat serialization 这个历史包袱。第三，它把 monitorability 和 security 也放进了表示结构里：显式分离不同来源的流，比在单流里靠格式 token 提示角色边界更干净。这些点对后续做 native agent model、latent / auxiliary stream、以及更结构化的 post-train 数据设计都有参考价值。不过从预训练研究的量级看，它更像是一个有意思的接口范式和 instruction-tuning 方案，而不是改变基座训练主线的工作。","technical_approach":"方法核心是把原本串行的 message format 改写成一个按时间步对齐的多列表格，每一列对应一种角色或通道，例如 user、system、assistant、thinking，甚至额外的 monitor stream。每次 forward pass 同时读取所有已有流的历史，并为多个输出流同时预测下一行 token。这样模型不再把不同角色拼成一条长序列，而是在共享上下文下做跨流条件建模。\n\n从实现上看，关键不是简单地“并行解码多个序列”，而是让不同流之间存在相互依赖的注意力结构。文中强调这是 instruction-tuning 层面的改造：已有 chat 数据可以被重排成多流监督格式；额外的 stream 数据也可以由现有 chat model 合成。空槽位用特殊空预测处理，推理时这些空位可以跳过，从而减少 KV cache 占用。作者还区分了 input streams 和 output streams：前者可以持续接收外部流入 token，后者由模型持续生成，因此理论上可以实现边读边写、边想边做。\n\n这套设计和标准 chat tuning 的差别在于，chat tuning 依赖 role token 和顺序拼接来表达结构；multi-stream 则把结构直接编码到张量布局和注意力关系里。和把工具调用、CoT、状态消息继续塞进单流相比，多流格式更接近一个原生的交互式状态机。论文还特别强调额外内部流的价值：它们不必承担对用户可见的解释责任，因此可以承载更自然的“次级自述”或意图暴露，这和把所有中间思考都压进一个公开 CoT 通道不同。","experiments":"全文给出的实验重点不在语言建模困惑度，而在系统行为层面。作者声称多流推理能显著降低 time-to-first-token，并在端到端延迟上受益，因为读取、思考、行动可以重叠；同时由于 LLM inference 常常是 memory-bound，多流并行输出不会按流数线性拖慢生成速度。任务性能方面，结论是大体保持不变，没有因为格式改写而明显退化。\n\n安全性实验围绕 prompt injection robustness：显式区分 system / user / self-generated streams 后，模型更容易识别“这段内容来自哪里”，因此在长上下文和混杂来源输入下更稳。监控性实验则观察额外内部流中是否会出现 situational awareness 或意图表达；作者的结论是，一些在可见输出或主 reasoning stream 中不明显的内容，会在附加内部流里更直接地出现。\n\n不过从基座研究者视角看，这篇的实验更像系统原型验证。若要判断其长期价值，最该追问的是：1）多流格式在不同模型规模下是否稳定；2）训练数据构造是否引入 teacher bias；3）多流 attention 的实现成本、cache 组织和吞吐收益在真实 serving 栈里是否成立；4）对标准 benchmark 和 agent benchmark 的收益是否来自结构本身，而不是额外监督信号。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 和 “The Advantages of Multiple Parallel Streams”，把问题设定吃透：作者真正批评的是单流 chat interface，而不是 Transformer 分钟可以抓住主线；如果你关心 agent-native pretraining 或 structured post-training，可以再细看数据构造细节和推理实现。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a structural bottleneck in chat-style LLMs rather than core language modeling accuracy. In standard chat formatting, user input, model output, internal reasoning, tool calls, and system messages are all serialized into a single token stream. That forces the model to read, think, and act one after another. It cannot react while generating, cannot generate while still ingesting new information, and cannot cleanly separate sources of information in long contexts. The authors argue that many latency, usability, security, and monitorability issues in modern agents come from this single-stream serialization.","importance":"For foundation-model research, the main value is conceptual. The work reframes instruction tuning from single-sequence chat imitation to structured multi-stream token prediction. That is not a new pretraining objective, but it does change the interface between the model and an agent environment. It also makes a useful point: some agent limitations may be caused less by model capability and more by the inherited chat protocol. A second contribution is representational hygiene. Separating user, system, model, and internal streams at the modeling level gives a cleaner signal than relying on role tags inside one long sequence. Still, the contribution sits closer to model interface design and post-training format design than to core pretraining advances.","technical_approach":"$42","experiments":"$43","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction and the section motivating the advantages of multiple streams. The central claim is about the limitations of the single-stream chat interface, not about replacing Transformers. Then read the method section carefully: data conversion from message logs, treatment of empty slots, the distinction between input and output streams, and the attention dependencies across streams. Those details determine whether this is merely formatting or a meaningful modeling change.\n\nIn the experiments, focus on three parts: latency and TTFT, prompt-injection robustness, and the qualitative analysis of auxiliary internal streams. However, acquiring high-quality reasoning traces is often costly and time-consuming. Hence, the self-improvement paradigm has emerged, enabling MLLMs to self-generate reasoning traces for training without external supervision. failure mode of RLVR-trained reasoners: they can solve clean math-style prompts but become brittle when the surrounding context contains irrelevant facts, misleading hints, or incidental correlations. Seirênes does not make the underlying task harder. It keeps the original problem and verifier fixed, and trains the model to reason correctly under adversarially generated contextual distractions.","importance":"The main value for foundation-model training is the framing of robustness as an online self-play signal rather than static data augmentation. Compared with systems such as R-Zero, Absolute Zero, or SPICE, which often create new tasks or supervision, Seirênes evolves the context around a fixed task. This targets a practical weakness in reasoning RL: models may learn shortcuts that work on clean benchmark distributions but fail when prompts contain plausible distractors. The contribution is best viewed as a post-training framework that can be inserted into an RLVR pipeline, with direct implications for how reasoning curricula are constructed.","technical_approach":"A single parameter-shared policy is prompted into two roles: a Reasoner and an Adversary. For each question, the framework builds a paired rollout bundle. First, the Reasoner solves the clean question and receives verifier-based rewards. Second, the Adversary generates plausible but misleading hints. Third, the Reasoner solves the same original question conditioned on each hint. This pairing isolates the causal effect of the adversarial context. Optimization builds on GRPO, using group-relative reward normalization instead of a learned critic, together with a clipped policy objective and a KL penalty. The Adversary is rewarded for exposing failures or derailing the Reasoner, while the Reasoner is rewarded for recovering the correct solution under interference. The test-time interface remains unchanged, so the trained model is intended to be a stronger standalone reasoner rather than a system that depends on a separate robustness module.","experiments":"The provided text reports consistent gains over standard RL and cooperative-hint baselines on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, with generalization to broader contextual perturbations. support：它尝试把训练样本从单点偏好扩展到多目标共现情境，而不是在固定样本分布上做权重平衡或参数插值。","experiments":"实验覆盖 8 个以上基线、4 组主要设置，包含 LLaMA-3、Mistral 以及 UltraFeedback、HelpSteer2 等偏好数据。论文报告 simultaneous alignment 下平均 overall reward 提升 4.6%；sequential alignment 中 helpfulness、harmlessness、truthfulness 等单偏好提升约 5%–12.4%，同时 harmlessness 保持在 97% 以上。图 1 中的整体分数显示 MORA 在多组设置里高于 SFT、DPO、MODPO、SPO、RSDPO、TSV-M、Knots、OrthAlign。例如 LLaMA-3 + UltraFeedback 中 MORA 为 83.0，高于 OrthAlign 的 75.2；Mistral + UltraFeedback 中 MORA 为 83.5，高于 OrthAlign 的 72.9。消融的关键应当是：只增加采样量不能补足缺失的 reward 维度；融合互补意图比单独优化单目标样本更有效。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 25–35 分钟。优先看 边界是否稳定、融合 prompt 的消融。需要留意两个风险：合成 prompt 是否引入不自然分布，以及评估是否过度依赖 reward model 或 judge model。","authors_note":"从给出的正文可确认通信作者包含 Models often become less helpful when optimized for harmlessness, and less safe when optimized for helpfulness. The authors attribute part of this ceiling to the low reward dimensionality of the prompts themselves, rather than only to the optimizer, parameter merging, or preference-weighting scheme.","importance":"The main value for pretraining-oriented readers is about signal design, not base-model pretraining. The paper suggests that the attainable behavior frontier depends on the reward support present in the data. If prompts expose only one preference dimension at a time, downstream optimization has limited opportunity to learn behaviors that satisfy multiple constraints simultaneously. This is relevant to SFT/RL data synthesis, reward-model data design, and the interface between pretraining and post-training.","technical_approach":"MORA changes the preference data distribution instead of primarily changing the DPO-style objective. It first uses model rollouts and reward analysis to identify prompts dominated by a single reward dimension. It then rewrites and fuses complementary intents into high-dimensional prompts. A typical example combines a benign chemistry question with a harmful bomb-making request, so the model must refuse the harmful intent while still answering the safe part. Compared with methods such as MODPO, SPO, RSDPO, OrthAlign, or model-merging approaches, MORA tries to expand the reward support of the training data rather than balance fixed objectives on the same prompt distribution.","experiments":"The paper evaluates against more than eight baselines across four main settings, including LLaMA-3 and Mistral with UltraFeedback and HelpSteer2. Under sequential alignment, it reports 5%–12.4% gains on individual preference dimensions such as helpfulness, harmlessness, and truthfulness, while keeping harmlessness above 97%. In Figure 1, MORA outperforms SFT, DPO, MODPO, SPO, RSDPO, TSV-M, Knots, and OrthAlign. For example, on LLaMA-3 with UltraFeedback, MORA scores 83.0 versus 75.2 for OrthAlign; on Mistral with UltraFeedback, MORA scores 83.5 versus 72.9 for OrthAlign. Prior work suggests that this degradation is related to changes in dominant singular subspaces of pretrained weight matrices. In this work, we propose preserving projected rotations in pretrained singular subspaces as an efficient proxy for Fisher-sensit","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-08T20:20:05+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_10973.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"全参数SFT会导致预训练权重的主导奇异子空间发生不必要旋转，破坏预训练阶段学到的通用能力，带来OOD泛化性能下降；而基于Hessian或Fisher信息的敏感方向识别方法在LLM尺度下计算成本过高，无法落地。","importance":"提出了一种计算高效、易集成的SFT正则化方法，有效平衡了SFT的领域内适应和领域外泛化的 trade-off，且能为后续RLFT提供更优的初始化，对后训练流程的设计有直接参考价值。","technical_approach":"提出旋转保留监督微调（RPSFT）：将预训练权重的前k个主导奇异向量张成的子空间作为Fisher敏感方向的高效代理，在SFT损失中加入正则项，惩罚该子空间内的权重变化。具体来说，对每个选中的权重矩阵，预计算其预训练状态下的前k个左右奇异向量基，将权重变化量投影到该子空间后计算Frobenius范数的平方作为正则项。与权重冻结、硬梯度投影不同，该方法仅锚定主导子空间，其余方向仍可自由更新，无需额外数据或任务边界，可直接集成到标准SFT流程中。","experiments":"在Llama和通义千问两个模型家族的不同尺寸模型上，基于OpenR1-Math数据做全参数SFT，将数学基准视为领域内任务，通用推理、安全、知识基准视为OOD任务：RPSFT相比 vanilla SFT和强SFT基线，一致提升了领域内-外的性能trade-off；分析显示RPSFT更好保留了预训练的表示几何结构；后续基于DAPO（GRPO的变体）做RLFT时，RPSFT初始化的模型最终RL性能始终更高或与基线相当。","reading_guide":"重点阅读第1节的问题背景和第3节的正则项设计，第4节的实验结果和几何漂移分析部分可快速浏览，附录中的LoRA对比可按需查看。预计阅读时间25-30分钟，核心关注正则项的构造逻辑和为什么主导奇异子空间可以作为Fisher敏感方向的代理。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Full-parameter SFT causes unnecessary rotation of the dominant singular subspaces of pre-trained weights, damaging general capabilities learned during pre-training and leading to degraded OOD generalization. Meanwhile, methods for identifying sensitive directions based on Hessian or Fisher information are computationally prohibitive at LLM scale and cannot be practically deployed.","importance":"It proposes a computationally efficient, easily integrable SFT regularization method that effectively balances in-domain adaptation and out-of-domain generalization during SFT, and provides better initialization for subsequent RLFT, offering direct reference for post-training pipeline design.","technical_approach":"Rotation-Preserving Supervised Fine-Tuning (RPSFT) is proposed: the subspace spanned by the top k dominant singular vectors of pre-trained weights is used as an efficient proxy for Fisher-sensitive directions, and a regularization term is added to the SFT loss to penalize weight changes within this subspace. Specifically, for each selected weight matrix, the top k left and right singular vector bases from its pre-trained state are precomputed, and the squared Frobenius norm of the weight change projected onto this subspace is used as the regularization term. Unlike weight freezing or hard gradient projection, this method only anchors the dominant subspace, leaving other directions free to update. It requires no additional data or task boundaries and can be directly integrated into standard SFT pipelines.","experiments":"Full-parameter SFT experiments are conducted on models of different sizes from both Llama and Qwen families, trained on OpenR1-Math data, with math benchmarks treated as in-domain tasks and general reasoning, safety, and knowledge benchmarks treated as OOD tasks: RPSFT consistently improves the in-domain/out-of-domain performance trade-off compared to vanilla SFT and strong SFT baselines. Per-token epistemic uncertainty is calculated as the mutual information of predictive distributions (entropy of ensemble average distribution minus the average entropy of individual adapters). The uncertainty signal is injected into the advantage function of policy gradient, and exploration pressure automatically increases as the policy converges. This is a practical abstraction for RLVR curricula, SFT data generation, and possibly large-scale algorithmic data mixtures in pretraining.","technical_approach":"EvoTD models a reasoning task along two axes: Algorithmic Skills, which specify the logical backbone such as binary search, heaps, or graph algorithms; and Complexity Attributes, which specify structural constraints such as input size, graph size, or the number of conditions. The framework introduces two evolutionary operators. Attribute Mutation scales or changes complexity attributes. Skill Crossover composes skills to discover new compound tasks. A proposer generates candidate tasks, and a solver learns from tasks that pass filtering. The key filter is based on the solver’s current policy and keeps tasks in the model’s zone of proximal development: not already trivial, but not impossible either. As the solver improves, the accepted task distribution shifts toward harder instances. Empirically, we observe a critical trade-off: certain fixed aggregations frequently suffer from training collapse, whi","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T12:45:03+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_12058.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决的是 GRPO 类算法中 token 概率聚合方式过于固定的问题：把 trajectory-level advantage 转成 token-level policy update 时，不同聚合函数会在稀疏信号利用和梯度方差之间产生明显取舍。","importance":"这篇论文属于 post-train/RL 优化方法，对预训练研究的间接启发在于训练信号如何被放大、平滑和稳定化。长链推理训练常见的问题不是只有 reward 设计，也包括 advantage 如何传递到每个 token。HölderPO 给出了一个统一视角，把 GRPO 的算术平均、GSPO/GMPO 的几何平均放进同一族聚合函数中，并用可调参数解释不同任务上的训练稳定性差异。它不是预训练方法，但对 RLVR 与 reasoning model 的训练动态有实用参考。","technical_approach":"方法核心是用 Hölder mean 统一 token-level probability ratio 的聚合。参数 p 控制聚合形态：p=1 对应算术平均，p 趋近 0 对应几何平均，其他正负取值则在两者之外提供连续调节。作者给出的分析是双向取舍：较大的 p 会把梯度权重集中到少数 token 上，更容易放大稀疏但关键的学习信号；代价是方差边界变松，训练更容易不稳。较小或负的 p 会压低方差，有利于收敛稳定；代价是会削弱稀疏高价值 token 的影响。基于这个观察，HölderPO 不是固定一个 p，而是采用动态 annealing：训练早期用较大的正 p 强化稀疏信号，后期逐步转向负 p 以控制方差。","experiments":"主要实验基于 Qwen2.5-Math-7B。作者先展示不同任务对 p 的偏好不同：AIME 这类稀疏信号任务在 p=3 附近表现更好，MATH500 这类更密集信号任务在 p=-1 附近更稳。固定 p=3 时，AIME accuracy 达到 46.7%，高于文中列出的 43.3% 对照记录。使用动态 p annealing 后，五个数学 benchmark 的平均准确率为 54.9%，相对标准 GRPO 提升 7.2%。评测包括 AIME、AMC、MATH、Minerva 和 OlympiadBench。方法还在 ALFWorld 上报告 93.8% success rate，相对 GRPO 的 72.8% 有 28.8% 相对提升。需要关注的是，不同任务的 reward 密度定义、p schedule 的敏感性、以及是否在更大模型和更长训练预算下保持同样结论。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 20 分钟。先看引言和 Figure 1，理解 p 对 AIME24 与 MATH500 的相反影响。再看理论部分中梯度权重分布和方差边界的推导，重点判断结论依赖哪些近似。实验部分优先看 p 的网格消融、动态 annealing schedule、与 GRPO/GSPO/GMPO/PMPO 的对比。若用于复现实验，应重点检查 reward normalization、长度归一化、KL 约束、采样组大小和 p schedule，这些因素会显著影响结论。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a specific weakness in GRPO-style training: trajectory-level advantages must be mapped to token-level policy updates, and the fixed aggregation function used for token probabilities creates a trade-off between exploiting sparse learning signals and controlling gradient variance.","importance":"This is a post-training optimization paper, but it is relevant to researchers studying training dynamics for reasoning models. It shifts attention from reward design alone to how reward-derived advantages are distributed over tokens. HölderPO provides a unified lens for arithmetic-mean GRPO and geometric-mean variants such as GSPO or GMPO, and explains why different reasoning tasks may prefer different aggregation regimes.","technical_approach":"The method replaces a fixed token-probability aggregation rule with the Hölder mean. The parameter p controls the aggregation shape: p=1 recovers the arithmetic mean, p approaching 0 recovers the geometric mean, and other positive or negative values provide a continuous family of update rules. The paper characterizes a two-sided trade-off. Larger p concentrates gradient weight on a smaller set of tokens, which can amplify rare but informative signals in sparse-reward reasoning tasks. The cost is a looser variance bound and higher risk of unstable updates. Smaller or negative p tightens variance and improves stability, but it can smooth away rare high-value token contributions. The proposed practical instantiation uses p annealing: start with a larger positive p to exploit sparse signals, then move toward a negative p to stabilize convergence.","experiments":"The main experiments use Qwen2.5-Math-7B. The ablations show task-dependent sensitivity to p: AIME-style sparse-signal tasks prefer larger positive values, with AIME24 peaking around p=3, while denser tasks such as MATH500 prefer lower or negative values, with a reported peak around p=-1. With fixed p=3, AIME accuracy reaches 46.7%, compared with the 43.3% reference reported by the authors. With dynamic p annealing, HölderPO reaches 54.9% average accuracy over five math benchmarks: AIME, AMC, MATH, Minerva, and OlympiadBench. This is reported as a 7.2% relative gain over standard GRPO. The method also reports a 93.8% success rate on ALFWorld, compared with 72.8% for GRPO. The main points to verify are reward-density definitions, sensitivity of the p schedule, and whether the same behavior holds for larger models and longer RL runs.","reading_guide":"A 20 minute skim is appropriate. Recent direct preference optimization approaches simplify training but incorporate safety through ad-hoc modifications such as multi-stage procedures or heuristic margin terms, lacking a principled derivation. We show that the likelihood ratio of the optimal safe policy admits a closed-form decomposition that reduce","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T16:19:42+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_12339.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文试图把安全对齐中的 helpfulness-safety 约束问题改写成一个密度比匹配问题，从而避免 Safe RLHF 那类多阶段流程，也避免 SafeDPO 等方法中较经验化的 margin 或重标注设计。","importance":"它对预训练研究的直接影响不大，但对 post-train 方法论有一定价值，尤其是 DPO 系列方法的统一理解。核心贡献是给安全偏好优化一个更干净的推导路径：从 KL 正则约束下的最优安全策略出发，推导策略 likelihood ratio 的分解，再用 Bregman divergence 做 ratio matching。这样的表述有助于比较不同 direct alignment loss 的差异，也有助于理解安全惩罚强度、参考模型和偏好数据分布之间的关系。","technical_approach":"论文从 constrained alignment 出发：最大化 helpfulness，同时满足 safety 约束。传统 Safe RLHF 需要 reward model、cost model、在线 RL 和 primal-dual 更新；DPO 简化了 helpfulness 对齐，但安全项往往通过额外阶段、辅助模型或 margin 加进去。BSO 的关键观察是，最优安全策略的 likelihood ratio 可以分解为三部分：reference model ratio、helpfulness preference ratio、safety correction term。于是训练目标变成让模型 ratio 匹配数据 ratio。作者进一步用一族 Bregman divergence 来度量 ratio 差异，不同凸生成函数 h 对应不同 loss。这样得到的 BSO 是单阶段训练方法，不需要额外 reward/cost model，只比 DPO 多一个主要安全强度超参数。论文还声称 SafeDPO 可被看作该框架下的一个特例。","experiments":"从提供的正文可见，论文在多个安全对齐 benchmark 上做了实验，并包含两个关键消融：安全惩罚强度，以及 Bregman generator 的选择。摘要和引言没有给出具体数值，因此不宜把效果幅度说得过满。可以确认的实验结论是：作者试图证明 BSO 在 helpfulness 和 safety 之间取得更稳定的折中，并且不同 generator 会影响训练行为。精读时应核对主表中相对 DPO、SafeDPO、Safe RLHF 或其他安全 DPO 变体的提升，以及拒答率、无害性、帮助性是否同时报告。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 30–40 分钟；如果正在做 DPO/RLHF loss 设计，可以精读理论部分。优先看 Background 中 DPO 的重参数化，再看 BSO 推导里最优安全策略 ratio 的三项分解。实验部分重点看安全强度系数的曲线，因为它决定方法是否只是通过更强拒答换取安全分数。还应检查不同 Bregman generator 的消融，看论文是否给出可操作的选择准则，而不是只给一个经验最优配置。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper reformulates safety alignment as density-ratio matching. It targets the gap between principled constrained RLHF, which is operationally heavy, and direct preference methods for safety, which often add safety through heuristic margins, relabeling, or multi-stage training.","importance":"The contribution is mainly methodological for post-training rather than base pretraining. It provides a cleaner way to derive safety-aware DPO-style objectives from an optimal safe-policy ratio. This is useful for researchers designing alignment losses, because it connects the reference policy, helpfulness preferences, and safety correction in one expression instead of treating safety as an extra penalty bolted onto DPO.","technical_approach":"BSO starts from constrained alignment: optimize helpfulness under a safety constraint. The key derivation shows that the likelihood ratio of the optimal safe policy decomposes into three interpretable factors: the reference-model ratio, the helpfulness preference ratio, and a safety correction term. Once written this way, training can be cast as matching the model ratio to the target data ratio. The authors then minimize Bregman divergences between these ratios. Each convex generator induces a different single-stage loss. The resulting methods require no separate reward model, no cost model, and no online RL; compared with DPO, the main additional control is the safety penalty strength. The paper also positions SafeDPO as a special case within this ratio-matching family.","experiments":"The provided text states that the method is evaluated on safety-alignment benchmarks, with ablations over the safety penalty and the choice of Bregman generator. The excerpt does not include exact numbers, so the reported performance should be checked directly in the experimental tables. It reframes adaptation as a multi-channel optimization problem rather than pure weight updates. This matters for continual pretraining and RL-based training because it suggests that some short-horizon task signal should remain in an external, cheap-to-update textual channel instead of being permanently absorbed into parameters.","technical_approach":"Fast-Slow Training separates slow parametric weights θ from fast textual weights ϕ. The slow side is optimized with RLVR following a ScaleRL-like recipe: group rollouts per query, verifier-based rewards, group-relative advantages, and a truncated importance-sampling REINFORCE/CISPO objective. The fast side is optimized with GEPA, which maintains a population of prompts, uses rollouts to obtain natural-language critiques from a frozen reflection model, and proposes textual mutations. FST interleaves these two optimizers. Instead of training a policy first and tuning the prompt afterward, the prompt population and the policy co-evolve. The method keeps multiple prompts because GEPA returns a Pareto frontier; different prompts can work better on different parts of the task distribution.","experiments":"The experiments cover RLVR settings in math, code, and general reasoning. However, existing studies largely attribute this advantage to denser and more stable supervision, while the parameter-level mechanisms underlying OPD's efficiency remain poorly understood. In this work, we argue that OPD's efficiency stems from a form of ``foresight'': it establishes a stable update trajectory toward the final model early in training. This foresight manifests in two aspects","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T08:19:15+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_11739.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的不是“OPD 比 RL 更稳”这种现象层问题，而是更细一层：为什么 on-policy distillation 在参数更新层面能更快到达有效解，且这种效率差异具体体现在哪些模块、哪些更新方向上。作者把问题拆成两部分。第一，模块分配问题：同样大小的参数更新，为什么 OPD 带来的推理能力增益更高。第二，方向演化问题：为什么 OPD 在训练早期就能沿着接近最终解的方向前进，而 RL 往往需要更长的探索和修正。论文给出的中心结论是，OPD 的效率来自一种可观测的“foresight”：它会更早识别低边际收益模块，避免把更新浪费在功能冗余区域；同时它的更新更早收缩到少数稳定主方向，并且这些主方向与最终解的主子空间高度对齐。","importance":"它的价值主要不在于提出一个全新的 post-train 框架，而在于把“蒸馏更容易优化”这件事从经验判断推进到参数动力学解释。对预训练研究员来说，这有三层启发。第一，训练效率不只是监督更密、更低方差，还和更新是否尽早进入正确子空间有关，这和预训练里常见的 low-rank update、module specialization、mid-layer importance 等现象是连着的。第二，论文把 RL 与 OPD 的差异落到模块边际效用和谱结构上，这为分析 SFT、RL、distillation、continual pretrain 之间的训练轨迹差异提供了可复用工具。第三，作者基于这个观察提出 EffOPD，用简单线性外推就能加速，说明如果一个训练范式早期方向已经足够稳定，那么后续很多算力可能只是沿既定方向缓慢推进。这对 cooldown、annealing、early stopping、trajectory extrapolation 都有直接启发。","technical_approach":"方法部分分成“解释”与“利用”两层。解释层面，作者先统一起点，用同一个 base model 分别做 RL 和 OPD，把最终参数差写成 ΔW。随后做了几类分析。其一是 norm-controlled scaling：固定最终更新方向，只缩放更新幅度 α，比较在相同更新范数下的性能曲线。结果显示 OPD 在同等 norm 下带来的推理增益更高，说明 RL 的 ΔW 中包含更多与任务弱相关的分量。其二是模块边际效用分析：按层、按模块统计 sensitivity 与 update norm，观察哪些区域“改得多但收益小”。作者发现 RL 与 OPD 对重要模块的敏感性排序并不完全不同，但 RL 会在低效模块上累计更大更新，尤其是一些对最终推理收益贡献有限的区域；OPD 则更早压低这些区域的更新，把预算集中到中间层等更关键模块。论文把这类现象称为 Functional Redundancy Avoidance。其三是更新方向的谱分析与子空间演化分析：对 checkpoint 间的更新矩阵做奇异值分解，考察能量是否集中在少数主方向，以及这些主方向与最终 checkpoint 更新子空间的夹角或对齐度。作者观察到 OPD 的更新更早呈现低秩集中，主子空间更稳定，且与最终解更早对齐；RL 的谱更分散，对齐更晚且波动更大。其四是早期 checkpoint 可恢复性实验：对早期 OPD checkpoint 做 module-wise norm scaling，发现只用约 10% 训练进度的方向信息，就能恢复约 80% 的最终推理性能。这是“早期已锁定主结构”的关键证据。\n\n在利用层面，作者提出 EffOPD。核心做法很直接：既然 OPD 的当前更新方向在早期就与最终方向高度一致，那么可以沿当前方向做线性外推，减少实际训练步数与样本数。它不是加新模块，也不是改 loss，而是一个 plug-and-play 的训练加速策略。论文强调它与现有加速手段正交，意味着可以叠加在已有 OPD pipeline 上。这里真正值得注意的不是线性外推本身，而是它成立的前提：训练轨迹必须足够稳定、方向漂移足够小。作者用前面的谱与子空间分析为这个前提提供了证据。","experiments":"实验覆盖 1.5B 到 32B 参数规模，包含 pretrained、SFT-tuned 和 thinking 系列模型；RL baseline 包括 PPO、GRPO、DAPO，OPD 则使用 pattern-aligned teacher，通常来自同家族更强模型。关键结果有四类。第一，在最终 checkpoint 上做方向固定、幅度缩放时，OPD 在相同 update norm 下取得明显更高的 reasoning gain，支持“不是更新更大，而是更新更有效”。第二，模块分析显示 RL 在低边际收益模块上累积了更多冗余更新，而 OPD 更集中于中间层等对推理更关键的区域。第三，谱分析显示 OPD 的更新具有更强 low-rank concentration，主子空间更早与最终解对齐；RL 的更新方向更分散，后期还在持续修正。第四，EffOPD 在 1.5B–32B 多个设置上平均带来约 3× 训练加速，同时保持与 vanilla OPD 可比的最终性能。摘要和引言里还给出一个很关键的数字：对 OPD 而言，只用约 10% 训练进度的 checkpoint，经 module-wise norm scaling 后即可恢复约 80% 的最终 reasoning performance。这说明早期方向信息的含金量很高。\n\n如果精读正文，建议特别留意作者是否报告了不同任务、不同 teacher-student gap、不同 RL 算法下子空间对齐曲线的一致性，以及 EffOPD 对超参的敏感性。因为这决定了“foresight”究竟是 OPD 的普遍属性，还是只在 pattern-aligned、同家族蒸馏里最明显。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合带着问题读，而不是顺着叙述读。先看 Figure 2 对应的 norm-controlled scaling 实验，确认作者要证明的不是“OPD 更新更小”而是“同样的更新量更有效”。再看 Functional Redundancy Avoidance 那一节，重点看层/模块粒度的 sensitivity 与 update norm 是否真的解耦，以及哪些模块被判定为低边际收益。然后看谱分析和子空间演化图，关注两个问题：一是 OPD 的低秩集中是否在训练很早就出现；二是“与最终解对齐”是用什么度量定义的。最后看 EffOPD，判断它到底是稳定可复现的工程技巧，还是高度依赖特定训练曲线形状。\n\n如果只有 shift、SFT 冷启动这些场景里。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a more specific question than the usual claim that OPD is simply easier to optimize than RL. It tries to identify the parameter-level mechanism behind that efficiency gap. Why does on-policy distillation reach a useful solution faster, and where exactly does the difference appear in the update trajectory? The authors decompose this into two issues. First, module allocation: under the same update budget, why does OPD convert parameter movement into reasoning gains more efficiently? Second, update direction: why does OPD move toward a final useful solution much earlier, while RL spends more time exploring and correcting? Their answer is a notion of “foresight.” OPD suppresses updates in low-marginal-utility regions early, and its updates collapse early onto a small set of stable dominant directions that are already well aligned with the final solution subspace.","importance":"Its main contribution is explanatory rather than purely algorithmic. It turns the vague intuition that distillation benefits from denser supervision into a concrete statement about update geometry. For pretraining researchers, this matters for three reasons. First, training efficiency is not only about lower-variance supervision; it is also about entering the right update subspace early. That connects directly to observations in pretraining around low-rank adaptation, module specialization, and the importance of middle layers. Second, the paper offers a reusable analysis lens for comparing RL, distillation, SFT, and continual pretraining through module utility and spectral structure. Third, the proposed EffOPD result suggests that once a training paradigm locks onto a stable direction early, a nontrivial fraction of later compute may just be slow progress along an already identified path. That has implications for cooldown schedules, early stopping, trajectory extrapolation, and compute allocation.","technical_approach":"$46","experiments":"$47","reading_guide":"$48","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读 §更新子空间分析即可；如果早期子空间对齐稳定，这个诊断可迁移到 SFT/RL 的训练预算裁剪。","en":"Read the update-subspace analysis; if early alignment is stable, the diagnostic is reusable for pruning SFT/RL training budgets."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Yuchen Cai","Ding Cao","Liang Lin","Chunxi Luo","Xin Xu","Kai Yang","Weijie Liu","Saiyong Yang","Tianxiang Zhao","Guangzhong Sun","Guiquan Liu","Junfeng Fang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["on-policy-distillation","post-training","training-dynamics","low-rank-updates","reasoning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11739","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.11739","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.12288","title":"TokenRatio: Principled Token-Level Preference Optimization via Ratio Matching","abstract":"Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is a widely used RL-free method for aligning language models from pairwise preferences, but it models preferences over full sequences even though generation is driven by per-token decisions. Existing token-level extensions typically decompose a sequence-level Bradley-Terry objective across timesteps, leaving per-prefix (state-wise) optimality implicit. pairwise preference 数据，也能恢复一个在状态—动作层面更接近最优的策略。","importance":"对 post-training 方法研究，这篇文章的价值主要在理论建模和目标函数设计，而不是工程 recipe。它把 preference learning 从“整段回答谁更好”推进到“给定当前 prefix，下一个 token 的偏好比率应该怎样匹配”，这更贴近语言模型的生成机制。对预训练研究员的启发在于两点。第一，很多对齐目标之所以效率不高，不是数据不够，而是监督粒度和模型决策粒度不一致。第二，sequence-level preference 也许可以通过更好的 ratio matching 形式，间接恢复 token-level 最优性，这对未来把偏好信号并入 mid-training 或 reasoning-oriented continual training 有方法论意义。","technical_approach":"作者提出 TBPO，Token-level Bregman Preference Optimization。核心不是简单的 token-wise DPO，而是先假设一个 token-level Bradley–Terry 偏好模型：在给定 prefix 状态 s_t 时，不同 next-token action 的相对偏好由某个 token-level score 决定。然后作者把学习问题写成密度比估计，也就是去拟合“偏好动作相对参考分布的 ratio”，并用 Bregman divergence 族来构造目标函数。这样做有两个结果。第一，logistic/DPO loss 只是这个更一般框架下的一个特例，而不是唯一选择。第二，在合适的 score parameterization 下，可以给出 token-level optimality 的理论刻画。\n\n文中给了两个主要实例。TBPO-Q 用 state-action value Q 来定义 token-level score，更接近从局部动作价值出发建模偏好；TBPO-A 用 advantage A 来定义 score，更强调相对当前状态基线的增益。作者沿用 KL-regularized sequential decision 的视角，把 next-token generation 写成 token-level MDP，并借助参考策略下的闭式最优 Q/A 形式，把 sequence-level comparison 数据转成可训练的 token-level ratio matching 目标。与 TDPO、TISDPO 这类方法相比，差别不在于是否把 loss 分摊到 token，而在于它显式定义了 token-level 偏好模型，并证明最小化该目标时恢复的是 token-wise optimal policy，而不是仅仅改善序列级偏好概率。","experiments":"实验部分的主张有三类：一是 TBPO 在多个 benchmark、不同 backbone、不同任务上优于强基线；二是训练更稳定；三是相对基线更能保持 response diversity。你给出的全文片段没有包含具体表格和数值，因此不能可靠写出提升百分点、模型规模或数据规模。可以确认的是，作者把比较重点放在 DPO 及其 token-level 扩展上，而不是和 RLHF 做大规模算力对决；论文更像是在同等偏好数据条件下比较目标函数是否更贴近自回归决策。\n\n从方法结构推断，最值得关注的实验应包括：TBPO-Q 与 TBPO-A 的对比，说明 Q-formulation 和 advantage-formulation 哪个更稳；不同 Bregman generator 对训练行为的影响，说明 logistic 不是唯一合理选择；以及长回答或长推理任务上的收益，因为 token-level credit assignment 的优势通常会在长 horizon 更明显。如果完整论文里有 calibration、entropy 或 模型，只是把信号分配到 token。然后看 TBPO 的推导，重点盯三件事：token-level Bradley–Terry 假设是什么；密度比是相对谁定义的；Bregman divergence 为什么能导出一族可替换的 preference loss。接着看 theorem 和 optimality characterization，确认“恢复 token-level optimal policy”的条件是什么，是否依赖模型容量、参考策略支持集或偏好噪声假设。\n\n如果只想判断值不值得后续复现，预计 30 分钟左右足够。若要深入，建议重点看 TBPO-Q/TBPO-A 的差别和实验中的稳定性曲线，因为这决定它更像一个漂亮的理论包装，还是一个真正可落地替代 DPO 的训练目标。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a granularity mismatch in preference optimization. DPO and related methods optimize sequence-level preferences over whole completions, while autoregressive language models act one token at a time conditioned on a prefix. Existing token-level variants usually redistribute a sequence-level Bradley–Terry objective across timesteps, but they do not explicitly define what token-level preference optimality should mean at each state. The paper asks whether one can recover a policy that is locally preference-optimal at the prefix level while using only standard sequence-level pairwise comparison data.","importance":"Its main contribution is conceptual and objective-level rather than recipe-level. The paper is useful because it reframes preference learning as a token-level density-ratio problem instead of a whole-sequence classification problem. That matters for anyone thinking about alignment signals as training targets: if the supervision granularity does not match the model’s decision granularity, optimization can be statistically and behaviorally inefficient. For pretraining-oriented readers, the broader lesson is that sequence-level supervision may still induce token-level optimal behavior if the ratio-matching objective is designed carefully enough. That idea could matter beyond DPO, including mid-training or reasoning-oriented continual adaptation.","technical_approach":"$49","experiments":"$4a","reading_guide":"Read it as a theory-and-objective paper first, not as an engineering recipe. Then move to the TBPO derivation and focus on three questions: what exactly is the token-level Bradley–Terry assumption, how is the density ratio defined, and why does a Bregman-divergence formulation produce a family of usable preference losses. After that, read the theorem and optimality characterization carefully. The key issue is under what assumptions the minimizer corresponds to a token-level optimal policy.\n\nA quick read should take about 30 minutes. 这类参数更新方法相比，它把对齐从参数偏置转成生成时的模块化控制；与普通 activation steering 相比，它不是一次性加方向，而是随生成过程在线感知、判断和注入信号。","experiments":"论文报告在多个模型规模上验证 SVGT。主要结果是 harmful score 通常下降超过 70%，同时保持生成流畅性。实验重点放在安全行为改善和语言质量保持之间的权衡。由于给出的正文片段没有完整列出模型清单、数据集、训练 curriculum 和消融表，具体数值不宜过度展开。可确认的实验结论是：独立 value module 能在冻结主干的条件下提供稳定的安全引导，且没有明显破坏生成连贯性。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 20 到 30 分钟。重点看方法部分的 usually embedded into the backbone parameters or residual stream, but the residual stream is highly context-dependent and constantly reshaped by computation, making value signals fragile and inconsistently expressed.","importance":"This is more relevant to post-training and inference-time alignment than to core pretraining. Its useful signal for foundation-model research is architectural: some normative constraints may not be well served by being entangled inside the backbone representation. A separate control module, connected through an explicit interface, can be a cleaner way to study stable guidance without rewriting the base model.","technical_approach":"SVGT freezes the LLM backbone and adds an independent value module. The module reads hidden states from a chosen layer, extracts prompt and current-generation state vectors, maps them into a dedicated value space, and converts the resulting value signal into bridge tokens. These bridge tokens act as late-bound attention targets for the backbone during generation. The method has two main components: independent value modeling, which keeps value representations outside the backbone dynamics, and explicit behavioral guidance, which injects those signals through a learnable attention interface. Compared with RLHF or DPO, the alignment signal is not folded into the model weights. Compared with simple activation steering, the guidance is generated online and exposed through a structured token interface rather than a fixed vector perturbation.","experiments":"The paper reports experiments across several model scales. The main reported outcome is a reduction of harmful scores by more than 70% in general, while preserving generation fluency. paper addresses safety alignment for tool-using LLM agents, where failures are trajectory-level rather than response-level. An agent may execute an unsafe tool call, leak information, or follow injected instructions before ending with a harmless-looking final answer. Conversely, it may avoid risk by over-refusing benign tasks, which improves apparent safety while degrading utility.","importance":"The work is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it is relevant to post-training for general tool agents. Its main value is the shift from final-response supervision to on-policy trajectory supervision. This matters because offline preference labels or scalar safety rewards often miss intermediate actions and can push the policy toward broad refusal instead of safer task completion.","technical_approach":"FATE, short for Failure-Trajectory Evolution, runs the current policy on agent tasks and collects verifier-scored failure trajectories. For each failure, the same policy proposes multiple repairs conditioned on the task, the failed trajectory, and verifier feedback. The failed trajectory is not cloned; it is used as context for localized correction. The training signal is built with PFPO, Pareto-Front Policy Optimization. Instead of collapsing security, utility, over-refusal control, and trajectory validity into one scalar reward, PFPO selects non-dominated repair candidates and trains the policy toward those repairs through supervised warmup and subsequent optimization. Compared with RLHF or DPO, the supervision is trajectory-aware. Compared with runtime guards, it changes the policy itself. Compared with expert demonstrations, it does not require hand-written repair trajectories.","experiments":"The evaluation uses AgentDojo, AgentHarm, and ATBench, with comparisons across model families, model scales, evolution rounds, and baselines. AI control / monitor 这类数据稀缺但鲁棒性要求高的场景。更具体地说，作者想回答三个问题：第一，跨领域分类训练能否迁移到相邻但未见过的任务；第二，这种专门的 classification tuning 相比普通 instruction tuning 到底多了什么；第三，模型在迁移时会不会学到“数据分布上的捷径”，从而忽略新的分类 prompt。论文的核心发现是：有迁移，而且在相邻领域上迁移幅度不小；但也存在明显的 prompt-following failure，尤其是在数据域相同、分类规则变了的时候，模型会把训练时的标签规则硬套到新任务上。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接关系不如 tokenizer、scaling law 那么强，但它对 pretrain↔post-train token 都不计入 loss，这样训练目标集中在分类决策本身。作者区分了两种评测模式。1-token classification 直接输出 0/1，并用 P(1)/(P(1)+P(0)) 作为分数；thinking classification 则让模型先推理，再给 0 到 100 的置信分。数据包括 Chemical harm 50k、Cyber harm 50k、ControlArena 4k、IMDB 25k，以及 Covert Reasoning 1.5k。这里的设计有两个关键点。第一，作者故意把“相邻领域迁移”和“同域不同 prompt”分开测，前者检验语义迁移，后者检验是否真的在 follow prompt。第二，作者比较了 classification data 与 instruction-following data 的混合方式，发现简单混合效果不好，因为分类样本只有极少数 unmasked token，训练信号密度远低于普通指令样本，导致分类目标被稀释。于是他们尝试先做 classification training，再做 instruction training，这种顺序训练比 naive mixing 更能兼顾分类能力和 prompt-following。这个结论很像 curriculum / stagewise training 的经验版：不是数据都放一起就行，关键在于每类样本对 loss 的实际贡献。","experiments":"最重要的结果有四类。第一，跨相邻领域迁移有效。作者给出的代表性结论是：在“is this cyber harm?”上训练后，迁移到“does the AI do anything dangerous?”这类未见目标任务时，可以恢复约 75% 的直接在目标任务上训练所带来的提升。第二，在目标域数据很少时，加入相邻域数据仍然有帮助，即使数据配比很不平衡。第三，存在两类泛化失败，尤其是单任务训练后，模型会把训练任务的分类规则粘在相似 transcript 上，哪怕 prompt 已经换了；也就是说它学到的部分是 domain-conditioned labeler，而不是 prompt-conditioned classifier。第四，加入 instruction-following 训练能缓解这种失败，但简单混合不如分阶段训练，因为分类 token 在 loss 中占比太低。生成式迁移结果也很关键：Qwen3-8B 只在单 token cyber harm 分类上训练后，thinking classification 的 AUC 从 0.84 提升到 0.94；单 token 分类训练还能提高模型在 的表现，这更贴近部署需求。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先看实验设置，尤其是 1-token 与 thinking classification 的定义、masked loss 的做法、以及各数据集之间的关系。然后重点看 Section 4 的迁移结果和 4.6 的 generalization failures，这两部分最能说明“分类训练提升了什么，又破坏了什么”。如果只想抓住工程启发，再看 instruction data mixing 与 stage ordering 的实验即可，预计 30 分钟左右。更值得细看的点有三个：一是为什么 classification samples 的有效训练 token 太少，导致 naive mixing 稀释目标；二是单 token paper studies a practical question about using LMs as classifiers: when a model is fine-tuned on multiple classification tasks, each with its own prompt, how much of that training transfers to new domains and new prompts? The target use case is AI control and monitoring, where labeled data is limited but robustness matters. The authors ask three concrete questions: whether cross-domain classification training transfers to adjacent unseen tasks, how specialized classification tuning differs from ordinary instruction tuning, and whether the model learns the intended prompt-conditioned rule or instead overfits to domain-specific label patterns. The main finding is mixed: transfer exists and can be substantial on adjacent domains, but there are clear prompt-following failures, especially when the data domain stays the same while the classification rule changes.","importance":"This is not a core pretraining paper, but it is relevant to the pretrain–post-train interface. Many safety monitors, behavior classifiers, and reward-related filters rely on the 'LM as classifier' paradigm. The paper exposes a useful tension: specialized classification tuning improves discrimination, but it can also weaken general prompt following, making the model behave more like a memorized labeler than a flexible classifier. That has implications for post-training data mixture design, masking strategy, and stage ordering. Another useful result is that single-token classification training transfers to generative settings such as reasoning-based classification and summarization. That suggests the gain is not tied only to output format; it may reflect a deeper shift in representation or decision boundary. \\","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T17:00:53+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_12400.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"核心问题是 In OPSD, the model samples its own trajectories and then uses a privileged version of itself as the teacher. The mismatch is subtle: the teacher may condition on expert solutions or reflective context that the student will not have at test time, so its token-level distribution can become biased, templated, or overconfident.","importance":"The main relevance to pretraining-adjacent work is signal quality. Dense token-level supervision is not automatically better than sparse outcome rewards. If the teacher distribution is miscalibrated, dense supervision can amplify the wrong bias faster than RLVR. This matters for reasoning mid-training, RL-style pretraining, and synthetic reasoning data pipelines, where the training target often mixes verifiable outcomes with generated rationales.","technical_approach":"OGLS-SD calibrates the teacher logits using outcome information. This steering signal modifies the privileged teacher distribution before distillation, so the student still receives dense token-level guidance, but the guidance is constrained by correctness signals. SFT、RL、过程监督、可解释性评估和安全审计都在不同程度上依赖可见推理轨迹。如果模型已经在隐状态里确定答案，却继续生成看似还在思考的文本，那么 CoT 作为监督通道就存在系统性噪声。对预训练和 post-train 接口的影响很直接：长 CoT 数据不一定提供真实的计算过程；基于 trace 的 reward 可能奖励表演式文本；过程监督若只看文本步骤，可能把描述性后验解释误当成因果计算。论文的贡献量级不在于提出新训练算法，而在于给出一个 step-level 测量框架，并用多种 probing 和 causal check 支撑这个测量。","technical_approach":"方法叫 Detect-Classify-Compare。第一步是把生成的 CoT 切成推理步骤，使用基于换行、转折标记和句界的规则解析器，解析 F1 为 0.87。第二步是在每个 step boundary 读取模型内部是否已经形成答案承诺。主要代理信号是 logit lens：把各层 hidden state 经过 unembedding 和 layer norm 投到词表空间，观察 ground-truth answer token 的概率 p_ans。若 p_ans 大于阈值 τ=0.3，就视为内部已经 committed。第三步是构造外显 CoT 标签：检查到当前步骤为止的累计文本中，是否已经出现 ground-truth answer string 或归一化变体。Belief-CoT Agreement 用来衡量内部承诺状态和外显答案到达状态是否同步。第四步是把不一致事件分成多个类型，其中最主要的是 confabulated continuation：内部答案已经稳定，但文本还在继续生成像推理一样的内容。第五步是比较 architecture-matched 的 Qwen2.5 和 DeepSeek-R1-Distill 模型对，观察 reasoning distillation 如何改变不一致模式。论文还用 Patchscopes、tuned lens 和 causal direction ablation 交叉验证 logit lens 读数，避免把单一 probe 的偏差误当成内部承诺。","experiments":"实验覆盖 9 个模型和 7 个 reasoning benchmark。核心结果是，latent commitment 与 explicit answer arrival 的 step-level 对齐平均只有 61.9%。在检测到的不一致事件中，58.0% 属于 confabulated continuation，也就是模型内部答案已稳定，但 trace 仍继续输出审慎推理的样子。logit lens 的可靠性做了多重检查：Patchscopes 在 mid-depth steps 上的一致率为 75.6%，在 final-layer steps 上为 74.7%；logit-lens-only disagreement 接近 0，mid-depth 为 0.0%，final layer 为 1.5%；tuned-lens 二分类在 4 个 held-out model-benchmark 配置上的一致率不低于 99.2%。Qwen2.5 与 DeepSeek-R1-Distill 的受控比较显示，reasoning pipeline 不只改变整体对齐率，也改变错误构成；在 32B 规模上，confabulated steps 下降，但 contradictory states 上升。论文还报告了一个重要行为关联：step-level alignment 越低，CoT utility 往往越大；配对截断和 donor corruption 实验显示，很多 post-commitment 文本更像描述性延续，而不是生成最终答案所必需的计算。","reading_guide":"建议精读方法和实验。先看引言中对 oversight channel 的定义，明确论文不是一般讨论 CoT faithfulness，而是在测量答案形成时间与文本呈现时间的偏差。方法部分重点看 logit lens 的 answer-commitment proxy、τ=0.3 阈值、BCA 指标、CoT 显式标签构造，以及 step segmentation 的误差来源。实验部分重点看 Figure 1、模型和 benchmark 覆盖、五类 mismatch taxonomy、Qwen2.5/R1-Distill 配对比较、截断实验和 donor corruption。附录 J.1 的 Patchscopes 与 tuned lens 验证值得看，因为整篇论文的可信度很大程度依赖内部承诺读数是否稳健。读的时候要注意一个限制：p_ans 是对 ground-truth answer token 的代理读数，它适合选择题或短答案任务，但不等价于完整的语义信念；对开放式推理和多 token 答案，需要更复杂的 commitment 定义。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether visible chain-of-thought traces are temporally synchronized with the computation that determines the answer. The key question is not whether CoT improves accuracy, but whether each written step reflects when the model has internally committed to the final answer.","importance":"This is a strong read for researchers working on reasoning training, process supervision, and CoT-based oversight. Many current pipelines use reasoning traces as SFT targets, reward-model inputs, verifier evidence, or safety-audit artifacts. If the model often commits internally before the trace reaches the answer, then the trace is a noisy supervision channel. Long rationales may train models to produce plausible deliberation rather than expose causal computation. The paper’s main contribution is measurement rather than a new training recipe: it provides a step-level framework for timing internal answer formation and supports it with probing and causal validation.","technical_approach":"$4f","experiments":"$50","reading_guide":"Read the method and validation sections carefully. We propose masked reconstruction as an alternative paradigm, where every token is scored using full bidirectional context. We introduce DiffScore, an evaluation framework built on Masked Large Diffusion Language Models. By measuring text recove","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T06:27:36+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_11601.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"用 AR LM 的 left-to-right log-likelihood 做文本质量评估时，早期 token 只能看到左侧上下文，导致“位置偏置”（positional bias）与“方向不对称”（directional asymmetry）：评分里混入了架构因果掩码带来的信息不充分，而不完全是文本本身质量。论文要解决的是：能否用一种与生成质量更对齐、且天然双向的打分范式，替代 AR likelihood 作为通用 NLG evaluator。","importance":"对预训练研究的价值不在于提出新训练目标，而在于把 diffusion/masked LM 的一个优势落到可量化的评估框架：同一段文本在不同 masking rate 下的“可恢复性”形成从局部流畅到全局一致的层级信号。这对两类工作有启发：\n- diffusion/any-order LM：提供了一个更贴合其预训练目标（masked reconstruction/ELBO）的下游使用方式，而不是强行用 AR likelihood 的指标体系。\n- 数据与训练动态：multi-timestep profile 把质量拆成不同上下文缺失程度下的曲线，可能用于诊断数据噪声、模板化、以及模型在局部/全局约束上的学习进度（虽然论文主要用于评估而非训练）。","technical_approach":"DiffScore 把评估定义为 masked reconstruction：对候选文本在不同遮蔽强度下进行破坏，让模型用双向上下文去恢复被 mask 的 token；高质量文本应在多种 mask pattern 下都更“可恢复”。实现依托 Masked Large Diffusion Language Models（MDLLM），其 forward 过程是把 token 以概率 t 替换为吸收态 [M]，reverse 过程用无因果 mask 的 Transformer 预测被遮蔽位置。\n- DiffScore-Zero：零样本，用 MDLLM 的 ELBO 作为分数。关键细节是 ELBO 里有 1/t 的重加权，用来抵消不同 t 下被 mask token 数量期望（约 tL）的变化，使不同 timestep 的贡献更可比。\n- DiffScore-FT：不依赖 gold reference 的任务相关微调（论文强调“无参考”以隔离评估范式本身的贡献），把 evaluator 适配到特定任务分布。\n- 诊断工具 1：multi-timestep quality profiles，把总分分解到不同 masking rate（t）上，形成曲线；低 t 更像局部流畅/短程一致性，高 t 更像全局结构/语义连贯。\n- 诊断工具 2：bidirectional PMI decomposition，用双向条件概率构造 PMI 风格分解，把 fluency 与 faithfulness（或覆盖）拆成更独立的维度；论文主张这是 AR 框架结构上做不到的，因为 AR 的方向性会把两者耦合进同一个因子分解里。\n与 BARTScore/GPTScore 的关键区别：不是换模型，而是换“评分因子分解方式”——从单一 left-to-right likelihood 变成跨遮蔽强度的可恢复性积分/分解，从而减少位置偏置与方向性伪影。","experiments":"在 10 个基准（覆盖 SFT or RL, can we predict which pretrained base model will yield the strongest post-trained model? The challenge is not ordinary base-model evaluation. A base model often cannot follow instructions well enough to be judged directly on open-ended tasks, creating a generation gap. At the same time, post-training targets are open-ended and multi-criteria, so simple exact-match or multiple-choice metrics do not capture the relevant capacity. rollout 重新采样，向量不对应任何离散 token，也不做梯度优化。作者把它作为软提示方法的对照组，用来检验“注入行为”本身的影响。机制解释分三步：第一，随机 embedding 作为从未见过的位置进入注意力，迫使模型在早期 token 上重新分配注意力；第二，各向同性采样覆盖多个方向，所以不同 rollout 会打开不同隐藏状态分支；第三，随着生成继续，KV cache 中真实生成 token 增多，随机前缀的相对注意力会自然衰减，输出轨迹趋于稳定。它与 Prefix-Tuning、Prompt Tuning、TTSV、SoftCoT、LTPO 的区别在于完全去掉训练阶段，只保留连续向量注入和独立重采样。","experiments":"文中报告了几个关键现象。第一，在 Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B 上，base model 使用不匹配 chat template 会损害推理，而追加 RSP 可恢复最高约 +29 个百分点，说明随机注入不是简单噪声扰动。第二，RSP 在早期解码阶段呈现与 LTPO、TTSV、SoftCoT 类似的 entropy signature，即前几个生成 token 的分布被明显打开。第三，Pass@N 的收益依赖每次 rollout 独立重采样；如果固定同一段随机向量，多样性累积效果会弱很多。第四，RSP 可以和 DAPO 或新推理能力。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether the gains from soft prompting come from learned continuous prompt content, or from the act of injecting non-vocabulary embeddings into the input stream.","importance":"The useful signal for pretraining research is about model behavior under out-of-distribution input embeddings. Random Soft Prompts have no learned semantics, yet can match optimized soft prompts in several math-reasoning settings. This suggests that continuous-vector injection can reshape early decoding distributions and create rollout diversity without parameter updates. The result is relevant to soft-prompt analysis, test-time compute, and RL rollout diversity, but it should not be read as evidence that the model has acquired new capabilities.","technical_approach":"RSP samples a sequence of continuous vectors from an isotropic Gaussian fitted to the entry-wise mean and variance of the pretrained embedding table, then appends those vectors to the input. Each rollout receives a fresh sample. The vectors are not discrete tokens and are never optimized. The paper uses this as a control for learned soft prompts. The proposed mechanism is attention-mediated: the random positions perturb early attention allocation, different Gaussian draws open different hidden-state branches, and the effect fades as generation proceeds because the KV cache becomes dominated by real generated tokens. Compared with Prefix-Tuning, Prompt Tuning, TTSV, SoftCoT, and LTPO, the method removes the training objective and keeps only continuous-vector injection plus independent resampling.","experiments":"The main reported findings are: on Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B, applying an unsuitable chat template to a base model hurts reasoning, while appending RSP recovers up to about +29 percentage points; RSP shows an early-decoding entropy pattern similar to optimized soft-prompt methods such as LTPO, TTSV, and SoftCoT; Pass@N improves only when RSPs are independently resampled across rollouts, indicating that diversity rather than fixed prompt content drives the gain; and RSP composes with DAPO training, suggesting that input-side stochastic branching can coexist with rollout-diversity optimization. We demonstrate that this practice produces substantially misleading rankings when two conditions co-occur: (1) the evaluation matrix is sparse and (2) items vary substantially in difficulty. We introduce a validity--diversity framework that attributes diversity collapse to how an LLM allocates probability mass across v","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-11T18:36:30+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_11128.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文关心的是：为什么扩大采样并不一定带来更多有效多样性。作者把问题定位到推理时的 next-token 分布，而不是只看最终生成文本的去重率或熵。核心结论是，LLM 的多样性瓶颈来自两类校准失败：有效 token 没有稳定排在无效 token 前面，称为 order calibration 问题；概率质量过度集中在少数有效 continuation 上，同时无效长尾仍占据大量概率空间，称为 shape calibration 问题。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发在于，它把“生成多样性不足”从解码技巧问题推进到模型分布质量问题。温度、top-k、top-p、min-p 这类方法只能重排或截断已有分布，无法修复有效候选在排序和概率形状上的错误。如果这些错误来自预训练数据分布、训练目标或后训练偏好压缩，那么单靠 decoding 很难解决。这个框架也提醒预训练评估不能只看平均 loss 或 benchmark accuracy，还需要看模型是否把概率质量合理分配给多个有效答案。","technical_approach":"作者建立了一个 validity-diversity 框架。validity 指模型分配给有效响应集合 V 的总概率质量；diversity 指在有效集合内部归一化后，分布的有效支持大小，具体用熵的指数形式刻画。这个定义有一个关键取舍：它假设所有有效响应同等可取，因此更适合用于存在明确有效集合的任务或可控诊断场景，而不直接等价于开放式写作质量。论文进一步把序列级问题拆到 token 级。order calibration 衡量有效 token 是否排在无效 token 之前；如果有效和无效 token 在排序中交错，任何基于排名的截断都会遇到取舍：截得窄会漏掉有效替代，截得宽会引入无效 token。shape calibration 衡量有效 token 内部的概率形状；如果少数头部有效 token 吃掉大部分概率，升温只会把质量从头部挪到长尾，其中相当一部分会流向无效 token，而不是流向低概率但有效的候选。作者还分析了局部校准错误如何随序列长度复合，解释为什么短步上的小偏差会在完整生成中表现为明显的模式坍缩。","experiments":"实验覆盖 14 个不同家族和尺度的语言模型。作者设计了可控诊断任务，其中有效集合可以被精确定义，并引入 oracle cutoff 作为参照，用来区分“解码规则没选好”和“模型分布本身不适合截断”。结果显示，常见 top-token filtering 方法无法同时恢复高 validity 和高 diversity：放宽截断会引入更多无效 token，收紧截断会丢失有效替代。温度采样也表现出类似限制，升温后概率质量并不会只流向罕见有效 continuation，而会大量进入无效尾部。论文还用实际生成例子展示了输出集中现象，例如 GPT-4 生成故事数据中有 59% 以 “Once upon a time” 开头。这类例子不是主要证据，但有助于说明序列级坍缩的外在表现。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 Introduction 和方法定义部分，重点看 validity-diversity 的形式化、order calibration 与 shape calibration 的区分，以及 oracle cutoff 诊断的设计。实验部分优先看比较不同 decoding 方法的图表，以及展示局部错误随长度累积的分析。预计阅读 25 到 35 分钟。值得深挖的问题是：这些校准失败是否能通过预训练数据去重、反模式数据平衡、熵正则、multi-answer 训练目标或后训练偏好数据改造来缓解。对只做解码策略的人，这篇论文的价值是诊断工具；对做预训练的人，更有价值的是它指出了 loss 之外的分布形状指标。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies why sampling more often fails to produce genuinely valid diversity. It moves the analysis from final-output diversity metrics to the model’s stepwise next-token distribution. The main claim is that diversity collapse comes from two calibration failures: valid tokens are not consistently ranked above invalid ones, and probability mass among valid continuations is too concentrated on a small head while invalid options occupy a heavy tail.","importance":"The useful point for pretraining research is that diversity is framed as a property of the learned distribution, not merely a decoding issue. Temperature scaling, top-k, top-p, and min-p can only reshape or truncate the distribution the model already provides. They cannot reliably recover valid alternatives if those alternatives are interleaved with invalid tokens or assigned very low mass. This suggests that pretraining and post-training should be evaluated not only by loss and task accuracy, but also by how probability mass is allocated across multiple valid continuations.","technical_approach":"The paper introduces a validity-diversity framework. Validity is the total probability mass assigned to the set of valid responses. Diversity is the normalized effective support size of the model distribution after conditioning on validity, using entropy to measure how broadly the model covers valid outputs. This setup assumes that valid responses are equally preferred, which makes it well suited to controlled tasks with known valid sets. The authors then define two token-level bottlenecks. Order calibration captures whether valid tokens appear before invalid tokens in the ranked distribution. When valid and invalid tokens are interleaved, any rank-based cutoff faces a direct trade-off. Shape calibration captures whether probability mass is spread across valid continuations or concentrated on a few head options. Raising temperature can reduce head concentration, but much of the released mass may flow into the invalid tail. The paper also analyzes how these local errors compound over sequence length.","experiments":"The empirical study covers 14 language models across multiple families and scales. The authors use controlled settings with exactly known valid sets and oracle cutoff baselines, which helps separate decoding failures from failures of the model distribution itself. The results show that common top-token filtering methods cannot reliably achieve both high validity and high diversity. Tight cutoffs preserve validity but discard valid alternatives; loose cutoffs recover some alternatives but admit invalid tokens. Temperature sampling has a related failure mode: increasing temperature does not selectively recover rare valid continuations, because probability mass also moves into the invalid tail. The paper also gives qualitative examples of output concentration, such as 59% of GPT-4-generated stories beginning with “Once upon a time.”","reading_guide":"Skim the introduction and formal setup first, especially the definitions of validity, diversity, order calibration, and shape calibration. Then read the diagnostic experiments with known valid sets and oracle cutoffs. The most useful figures are likely the ones showing ranked token distributions, validity-diversity trade-offs, and error compounding over sequence length. Estimated reading time is 25 to 35 minutes. The deeper pretraining question is whether these calibration failures can be reduced by data mixture design, multi-answer supervision, entropy-aware objectives, or post-training procedures that avoid over-compressing the output distribution.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"不改训练目标，但把 diversity collapse 拆成 order/shape calibration；只看框架和诊断实验，能指导内部校准评测。","en":"No new training objective, but the order/shape calibration split is a useful diagnostic; read the framework and experiments for calibration probes."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of Southern California"],"authors":["Amin Banayeeanzade","Qingchuan Yang","Dhruv Tarsadiya","Fatemeh Bahrani","Leonardo Blas","Alfy Samuel","Robin Jia","Meisam Razaviyayn","Sai Praneeth Karimireddy"],"notable_authors":["Robin Jia"],"tags":["calibration","decoding","diversity","sampling","LLM-analysis"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11128","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.11128","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.11608","title":"PRISM: A Geometric Risk Bound that Decomposes Drift into Scale, Shape, and Head","abstract":"Comparing post-training LLM variants, such as quantized, LoRA-adapted, and distilled models, requires a diagnostic that identifies how a variant has drifted, not only whether it has degraded. tell not only that the model degraded, but whether the drift came from activation scale, representation geometry, or the language modeling head.","importance":"PRISM is not a new pretraining method, but it is useful for studying model drift around the pretrain-to-posttrain interface. It turns representation comparison into a proxy bound on cross-entropy risk, which makes it more actionable than scalar similarity metrics such as CKA or SVCCA. For pretraining researchers, the same decomposition can help inspect checkpoint annealing, continual pretraining, low-bit training or quantization, LoRA-induced forgetting, and head-backbone mismatch.","technical_approach":"The method relies on two structural assumptions that are often reasonable for related LLM checkpoints: a nonlinear Transformer backbone followed by a linear lm_head, and near-isometric hidden representations that can be aligned with an orthogonal map. PRISM collects hidden-state matrices from a target model and a proxy variant on shared inputs, aligns them through an orthogonal Procrustes map, and decomposes the feature residual into a scale term Δρ and a shape term 1-Ω. It adds a third term, γ, which measures head divergence weighted by the proxy feature covariance. The risk bound is derived through a hybrid-risk construction and a sharper cross-entropy Lipschitz argument for the linear head. Instead of using the full spectral norm of the head, which becomes loose at vocabulary scale, the paper ties the constant to pairwise token-embedding distances. In frozen-head LoRA, the head term vanishes, and the differentiable trace form of the shape term can be used directly as a regularizer against backbone drift.","experiments":"The experiments cover Llama and Qwen variants, with additional Ministral and DeepSeek results in the appendix, over five benchmarks. For post-training quantization, the PRISM bound reaches a mean Spearman correlation of 0.820 with observed degradation. For LoRA forgetting, the mean Spearman correlation is 0.831. The axes separate different failure modes: Q2/Q3 quantization mainly distorts representation shape; cross-task LoRA drift is strongly reflected in scale separability; some Qwen3 Q6_K/Q8_0 cases show increased head divergence. The shape-based regularizer also reduces forgetting under LoRA fine-tuning without replay and outperforms replay-based baselines in aggregate. The bound is best read as a ranking and localization tool rather than a tight absolute-risk estimator.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 30 to 45 minutes. fo","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T17:09:41+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_12412.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"解释自由文本场景下的 的“假设空间”具体化为带度量的概念空间，并把“信念”定义为概念空间上的分布，从而得到一个更高一层的信念空间。\n1) 概念空间（conceptual space）：借鉴 Gärdenfors 的 conceptual spaces，把概念表示为低维属性空间中的区域，空间带有距离度量，距离刻画相似性。\n2) 概念信念空间（conceptual belief (e.g., story understanding), belief updates are often non-monotonic and idiosyncratic, and it is unclear what latent hypothesis space the model is effectively performing inference over. When a prompt is semantically underdetermined, the available premises do not specify a unique answer. In such cases, a model cannot always provide a decisive answer while also maintaining strong correctness and avoiding unsupported preferences.","importance":"This is not a pretraining method paper and it does not propose a new optimization procedure. Its relevance lies in alignment data design, refusal policies, clarification behavior, and reward modeling. Some failures that appear as hallucination, bias, or low helpfulness may come from the prompt lacking enough semantic constraints. If training data forces a single answer for underdetermined questions, it can teach hidden preferences. If training always rewards refusal, it reduces usefulness. A better target is often to state assumptions, give conditional answers, or ask for the missing decision criterion.","technical_approach":"The paper formulates a CAP-like trilemma. For a prompt x, it defines the available facts and premises F_x, an answer space A, and the set S_x of premise-compatible answers. If S_x has one element, the prompt has a singleton solution. If it has more than one element, the prompt is underdetermined. The three desiderata are strong correctness, meaning the answer is entailed by the available premises; strict non-bias, meaning the model does not privilege one candidate without premise-supported justification; and decisive utility, meaning the response gives a concrete and actionable answer. The formal argument uses two candidate answers: if the premises do not justify preferring either one, then any decisive singleton answer requires an additional criterion θ. If θ is not supplied by the user or entailed by the premises, it functions as an unsupported selection rule.","experiments":"There are no model experiments, ablations, training runs, or benchmark results. The comparative information generated by multi-candidate sampling and ranking during test phase is only used for inference, not reused for training, leading to the model continuously assigning high probability to plausible but incorrect code, which limits the improvement of generation capability. Meanwhile, existing methods lack a clear mechanism to transfer the discriminative ability of ranking learning back to generation capability.","importance":"This work proposes a primal-dual training paradigm for generation-judgment, and proves that reinforcement learning training only in the dual judgment space, without direct generation reward, can improve both the model's judgment capability and native generation capability. All supervision signals come from the correctness comparison of candidate pairs generated by the model itself, with no direct generation reward. 分数，而在于它把“推理过程是否真实”转化为可自动验证的密集监督。类似思路可以迁移到数学、工具调用、程序执行、环境交互等任务：不要只奖励最终答案，而要构造中间状态，使长链条推理有可分解的信用分配信号。","technical_approach":"方法有两层。第一层是执行轨迹构造：自动在程序关键位置插入结构化 print 锚点，例如变量更新、循环退出、return 主要在一组采样轨迹之间做相对奖励归一化，StepCodeReasoner 进一步加入轨迹内部的 step-level reward。它同时比较不同轨迹的整体好坏，也考察单条轨迹中某个中间预测是否帮助后续执行保持正确。这个设计针对的是长程序执行中的信用分配问题：早期变量状态错了，后面即使碰巧答对，也不应得到同样奖励。","experiments":"实验使用 7B 模型。平均分达到 87.8%，高于 benchmark 的细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The work addresses a specific failure mode in code reasoning training: models are rewarded for the final answer while their intermediate execution states remain unchecked. A model can reach the right output with an inconsistent simulation of variables, branches, or loops.","importance":"The main relevance is the training signal, not the code benchmark itself. StepCodeReasoner shows how to turn a long-horizon reasoning task into a sequence of automatically verifiable intermediate targets. This is useful for pretraining and post-training design because it points to a general pattern: dense, executable supervision can reduce reward hacking when final-answer rewards are too sparse.","technical_approach":"The method first instruments programs with structured print-style execution anchors at important program points, such as variable updates, loop exits, and return statements. These anchors expose runtime states and convert code reasoning into stepwise execution modeling. The task is split into output prediction and input prediction, each using prompts aligned with execution traces rather than plain assertion completion. On top of this data format, the authors introduce Bi-Level GRPO. It keeps group-relative comparison across sampled trajectories, while adding intra-trajectory shaping rewards for intermediate states. The intra-trajectory term rewards steps that are not only locally correct but also support later correct execution. This is the main difference from terminal-only RL or standard GRPO-style optimization.","experiments":"The reported 7B model reaches an average score of 87.8%, compared with 81.8% for CodeReasoner-7B. constraint","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T17:15:45+00:00","category_id":"coding_agent","cover_url":"/covers/2605_12421.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"当用 representational divergence：不同表示面导致正确率系统性分叉。\n- heuristic trap：效率导向提示触发的六类范式相关失败模式（文中描述为：引入不可靠界/近似、过拟合局部启发式、约束写错但看似更“聪明”、复杂化导致求解器退化或无解、以及迭代 refinement 仍保留错误结构等）。","experiments":"数据与设置：CP-SynC-XL，100 个组合问题、4,577 个实例；模型为 GPT-5.3-Codex、Gemini 3.1 Pro、DeepSeek-V3.2。\n\n主要结论（定性为主，论文在引言中给出方向性结果）：\n- Python + OR-Tools 在所有 LLM 上正确率最高；MiniZinc + OR-Tools 覆盖率更低；纯 Python 原生搜索 silent failure 最高。\n- heuristic 提示并不稳定优于 baseline：中位数可能略有加速，但收益呈双峰分布，且在较弱模型上可能出现“显著回退”。\n\n建议读者在正文中重点核对：各范式的正确率/覆盖率差距、超时与 silent failure 的占比、以及 heuristic trap 六类失败模式各自贡献了多少回退（这些比单一平均分更能指导‘该让模型做什么’）。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 40-60 Python API 更稳、为什么 heuristic 提示会把错误结构固化。\n- 如果只关心预训练启发：把注意力放在“模型对 API/DSL 的语料熟悉度”这一点，思考是否需要面向 OR-Tools/MiniZinc 这类形式化语言做持续预训练或中训覆盖。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In LLM-synthesized combinatorial solvers, should the model focus on formalizing constraints or also try to optimize search with heuristics, and how does the choice of representation surface (native Python search vs Python OR-Tools API vs MiniZinc) affect correctness and failure modes?","importance":"The pretraining-relevant signal is about representation and incentives. First, correctness is dominated by the modeling surface the LLM is fluent in: Python + OR-Tools beats MiniZinc even with the same CP-SAT backend, implying that corpus coverage and code/API familiarity can matter more than the underlying algorithmic power. Second, prompting for efficiency triggers a ‘heuristic trap’: the model introduces unverified bounds/approximations or overcomplicated constraints that look clever but reduce fidelity. This mirrors reward-design pitfalls in post-training: optimizing for proxy metrics (speed/clever heuristics) can systematically trade off correctness.","technical_approach":"They run a controlled factorial study with two axes: (1) solver-construction paradigm (Python; Python + OR-Tools CP-SAT API; MiniZinc + OR-Tools), and (2) prompt (correctness-only baseline vs efficiency/heuristic-oriented). The two solver-backed paradigms share the same CP-SAT backend, isolating the effect of the representation surface.\n\nEach LLM must generate a reusable solver artifact that reads instance parameters at runtime and outputs solutions in a fixed schema. An evaluation harness executes the artifact, validates outputs, and records correctness, timeouts, and silent failures. The paper emphasizes instance-level behavior and bimodality rather than only averages.\n\nKey notions:\n- Representational divergence: systematic correctness gaps driven by the modeling language/API.\n- Heuristic trap: efficiency prompting induces recurring failure modes (e.g., unjustified bounds, local approximations, constraint overcomplication) that iterative refinement often fails to remove.","experiments":"Benchmark: CP-SynC-XL with 100 problems and 4,577 instances.
